FILE: README.md
================================================
# SIPERPUS PNL

React 19 + Vite + Tailwind CSS v4 dengan Supabase sebagai sumber data dan autentikasi.

## Setup

1. Salin `.env.example` menjadi `.env` lalu isi `VITE_SUPABASE_URL` dan `VITE_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY` (atau `VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY`).
2. Pada project Supabase kosong, jalankan berurutan: `supabase/schema.sql`, `supabase/migrations/001_fixes.sql`, `supabase/migrations/002_catalog_taxonomy.sql`, `supabase/migrations/20261101232250_student_page_fixes.sql`, lalu `supabase/seed.sql`. Semuanya aman dijalankan ulang; seed memakai `on conflict`/lookup agar tidak menggandakan data.
3. Jika memakai Supabase CLI, jalankan `supabase db query --linked --file <file>` untuk setiap file dalam urutan di atas. Migrasi katalog menyediakan master jurusan dan tema bertingkat; migration student page memperbaiki PIN reservasi, batas file storage, dan job kedaluwarsa hold.
4. Setelah seed, upload PDF demo ke Storage dengan CLI (fitur Storage CLI masih experimental):
   `supabase storage cp --experimental --linked --content-type application/pdf public/demo-pdfs/senja-di-pelabuhan-krueng-geukueh.pdf ss:///repository-files/demo/senja-di-pelabuhan-krueng-geukueh.pdf`
   Ulangi perintah yang sama untuk dua file PDF lain di `public/demo-pdfs/`.
   File demo hanya untuk pengujian dan ditandai sebagai karya sintetis. Jangan menghapus atau menimpa file karya pengguna.
5. Jalankan `npm install` dan `npm run dev`.

## Akun demo

Di Dashboard Supabase buka **Authentication → Users → Add user**. Supabase tetap memerlukan email internal, tetapi pengguna login menggunakan NIM/NIP; email ini tidak perlu ditampilkan di UI:

- Mahasiswa: `2022573010041@siperpus.pnl.ac.id`, login dengan NIM `2022573010041`
- Pustakawan: `198503152010011001@siperpus.pnl.ac.id`, login dengan NIP `198503152010011001`
- Akun Ammar: `2024573010129@siperpus.pnl.ac.id`, login dengan NIM `2024573010129`, prodi `Teknologi Informasi dan Komputer`

Tetapkan password masing-masing. Setelah akun dibuat, buka **Edit user → Metadata** dan isi `app_metadata` pustakawan dengan `{"role":"librarian"}`. Jangan menaruh role librarian di `user_metadata`. Jalankan seed ulang untuk data demo.

### Student Demo Accounts

Seeder akun mahasiswa hanya boleh dijalankan secara lokal/development. Isi environment berikut tanpa menaruh service role key pada variabel `VITE_`:

```bash
export SUPABASE_URL="https://your-project.supabase.co"
export SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY="..."
export DEMO_SEED_ENABLED=true
npm run seed:demo-students
```

Password setiap akun demo mahasiswa adalah NIM masing-masing. Script tidak membuat duplikat, tidak berjalan saat `NODE_ENV=production`, dan memakai fixture ID deterministik untuk transaksi. Email internal hanya kebutuhan Supabase Auth; login tetap memakai NIM.

| Nama | NIM | Email internal | Program Studi | Skenario |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Mahasiswa Demo | 2022573010041 | 2022573010041@siperpus.pnl.ac.id | Teknologi Informasi dan Komputer | Denda belum dibayar |
| Siti Rahma | 2022573010042 | 2022573010042@siperpus.pnl.ac.id | Bisnis | Akun bersih / empty state |
| Rafi Akbar | 2022573010043 | 2022573010043@siperpus.pnl.ac.id | Teknik Elektro | Peminjaman aktif |
| Nur Aini | 2022573010044 | 2022573010044@siperpus.pnl.ac.id | Teknik Mesin | Peminjaman terlambat |
| Fajar Pratama | 2022573010045 | 2022573010045@siperpus.pnl.ac.id | Teknik Kimia | Reservasi aktif |
| Nadia Putri | 2022573010046 | 2022573010046@siperpus.pnl.ac.id | Teknik Sipil | Denda menunggu verifikasi |
| Dimas Saputra | 2022573010047 | 2022573010047@siperpus.pnl.ac.id | Teknologi Informasi dan Komputer | Pembayaran ditolak |
| Cut Nabila | 2022573010048 | 2022573010048@siperpus.pnl.ac.id | Bisnis | Riwayat dan denda lunas |
| Muhammad Fajri | 2022573010049 | 2022573010049@siperpus.pnl.ac.id | Teknologi Informasi dan Komputer | E-Repository multi-status |

Seeder juga mengunggah bukti QRIS sintetis bertanda DEMO, membuat buku fixture, transaksi, dan tiga karya E-Repository Fajri. Jalankan ulang command yang sama untuk mengembalikan seluruh skenario ke keadaan deterministik; akun yang sudah ada tetap memakai password sebelumnya.

## Validasi

```bash
npx tsc --noEmit
npm run build
```



================================================
FILE: AGENTS.md
================================================
# figma-make-app

React + Vite + Tailwind CSS project running inside Figma Make.

## Development Server

A Vite development server is **already running** on `$PORT` (default 8443). You don't need to start it manually.

- Preview URL: The user can access the running app through the preview panel
- Hot reload: Changes to source files are reflected immediately

## Project Structure

This is the canonical project structure. Start with task-relevant files below. Only follow imports or inspect other files when required, when a documented path is missing, or when the repository contradicts this guide.

- `src/main.tsx` - React entrypoint; imports `src/index.css` and mounts `src/App.tsx` into the `#root` element
- `src/App.tsx` - Primary application component and the usual starting point for UI work
- `src/index.css` - Global CSS entrypoint and Tailwind CSS v4 import
- `index.html` - Vite HTML shell containing the `#root` element and loading `src/main.tsx`
- `package.json` - Project dependencies and the Vite build, development, preview, and formatting scripts
- `vite.config.ts` - Vite configuration with React, Tailwind CSS v4, and Figma Make plugins plus the `@` alias for `src`
- `.mise.toml` - Toolchain versions for Node.js and pnpm

## Dependencies

- Runtime: React 19 and React DOM 19
- Styling: Tailwind CSS v4 with the `@tailwindcss/vite` plugin
- Build tooling: Vite 8, TypeScript 5.7, and `@vitejs/plugin-react`
- Formatting: oxfmt

## Styling

This project uses **Tailwind CSS v4** through the `@tailwindcss/vite` plugin configured in `vite.config.ts`. `src/index.css` imports Tailwind with `@import 'tailwindcss';`. Use Tailwind utility classes directly in JSX and put global CSS or Tailwind v4 theme customization in `src/index.css`. This scaffold does not need a Tailwind config file or PostCSS config.

`src/main.tsx` imports `src/index.css`, so global font wiring belongs in `src/index.css`. Keep CSS `@import` statements first, then add any `@font-face` rules and font-family defaults there.

## Code quality

- Use double quotes for strings containing apostrophes (`"We're here to help"`), or escape them in single-quoted strings. An unescaped apostrophe in a single-quoted string breaks the build.
- Ensure JSX tags are closed and braces are balanced.
- Export components as default exports.

# SIPERPUS-REPO - Project Context & Next Steps

This document provides a complete overview of the **SIPERPUS** (Sistem Informasi Perpustakaan Politeknik Negeri Lhokseumawe) application for AI models (Gemini CLI, OpenCode, Antigravity) and human developers.

---

## 1. Project Overview & Tech Stack
- **Framework:** React 19 + TypeScript (via Vite 8)
- **Styling:** Tailwind CSS v4 (`@tailwindcss/vite`)
- **Package Manager / Runtime:** `npm` (Node v20+)
- **Build / Lint Verification:**
  - TypeScript: `npx tsc --noEmit`
  - Vite Build: `npm run build`
  - Dev Server: `npm run dev`

---

## 2. Current Architecture & File Structure

The project has undergone a complete **Phase 1 Modularization** (all giant monolithic files were dismantled into clean, maintainable components):

```
src/
├── components/
│   ├── Navigation.tsx        # Top navbar & mobile drawer
│   ├── Footer.tsx            # Footer component
│   └── ui/                   # Reusable UI primitives (Button, Modal, Card, Stat, Badge, etc.)
│
├── pages/                    # Views and sub-views
│   ├── LandingPage.tsx       # Landing page (Hero, Stats, Featured, E-Repo preview, Testimonials)
│   ├── CatalogPage.tsx       # Public/Student physical catalog & BooksView
│   ├── RepositoryPage.tsx    # E-Repository search & RepositoryView
│   ├── HelpPage.tsx          # FAQ & contact info
│   │
│   ├── StudentDashboard.tsx  # Student Portal - Main Dashboard
│   ├── StudentCirculation.tsx# Student Portal - Active loans & hold history
│   ├── StudentERepo.tsx      # Student Portal - Upload and track thesis/papers
│   ├── StudentFines.tsx      # Student Portal - Fine overview, status Bebas Pustaka
│   ├── StudentShared.tsx     # Student Portal - Shared layout atoms and icons
│   ├── QrisMock.tsx          # QRIS payment modal simulation
│   │
│   ├── LibrarianDashboard.tsx # Librarian Portal - Metrics & operational summary
│   ├── LibrarianSirkulasi.tsx # Librarian Portal - Desk desk / NIM lookup & hold handovers
│   ├── LibrarianFines.tsx     # Librarian Portal - QRIS proof verification & clearance
│   ├── LibrarianCuration.tsx  # Librarian Portal - Paper curation (approve/revise)
│   ├── LibrarianCatalog.tsx   # Librarian Portal - Book list & stock manager
│   ├── LibrarianBookForm.tsx  # Librarian Portal - Add/edit book modal
│   └── LibrarianShared.tsx    # Librarian Portal - Shared layout atoms, metrics, and icons
│
├── data/
│   ├── mockData.ts           # Public catalog books, e-repository papers, FAQ data
│   ├── portalData.ts         # Student portal initial mock data
│   └── librarianData.ts      # Librarian portal initial mock data & helper functions
│
├── types/
│   └── index.ts              # Core shared TypeScript types (Book, RepositoryItem, etc.)
│
├── App.tsx                   # Top-level router / view switcher (Landing, Catalog, Repo, Student, Librarian)
├── StudentPortal.tsx         # Shell & state container for Student Portal
├── LibrarianPortal.tsx       # Shell & state container for Librarian Portal
└── main.tsx                  # App entrypoint
```

---

## 3. Progress Tracking

### Phase 1: Modularization & Decoupling (COMPLETE)
- [x] Removed Figma Make specific dependencies and HTML tags.
- [x] Initialized Git repository with clean commits.
- [x] Split `App.tsx` into modular UI primitives (`components/ui/`) and standalone pages (`pages/`).
- [x] Split `StudentPortal.tsx` into modular sub-views (`StudentDashboard`, `StudentCirculation`, `StudentERepo`, `StudentFines`).
- [x] Split `LibrarianPortal.tsx` into modular sub-views (`LibrarianDashboard`, `LibrarianSirkulasi`, `LibrarianFines`, `LibrarianCuration`, `LibrarianCatalog`, `LibrarianBookForm`).
- [x] Zero TypeScript errors (`npx tsc --noEmit` passes).
- [x] Production build passes (`npm run build` outputs clean bundle).

---

## 4. Phase 2: Supabase Integration Plan (UPCOMING)

When continuing this project, Phase 2 replaces client-side mock states with real Supabase persistence and authentication.

### Recommended Supabase Database Schema

1. **`profiles`**
   - `id`: UUID (references `auth.users`)
   - `nim`: text / varchar (unique, e.g. "2101020045")
   - `full_name`: text
   - `role`: enum (`student`, `librarian`)
   - `prodi`: text
   - `clear_status`: boolean (Status Bebas Pustaka)

2. **`books`**
   - `id`: uuid / int
   - `code`: text unique (e.g. "INF-042")
   - `title`: text
   - `author`: text
   - `category`: text
   - `year`: int
   - `stock`: int
   - `shelf`: text (e.g. "Rak A-03")
   - `cover_url`: text

3. **`holds`** (Reservasi Buku)
   - `id`: uuid
   - `user_id`: uuid (references `profiles.id`)
   - `book_id`: uuid (references `books.id`)
   - `pin`: text (e.g. "PNL-HOLD-8921")
   - `status`: enum (`active`, `collected`, `expired`, `cancelled`)
   - `expires_at`: timestamptz

4. **`loans`** (Peminjaman Aktif & Histori)
   - `id`: uuid
   - `user_id`: uuid (references `profiles.id`)
   - `book_id`: uuid (references `books.id`)
   - `borrowed_at`: timestamptz
   - `due_date`: timestamptz
   - `returned_at`: timestamptz (nullable)
   - `status`: enum (`borrowed`, `returned`, `overdue`)

5. **`fines`** (Denda Keterlambatan & Pembayaran QRIS)
   - `id`: uuid
   - `user_id`: uuid (references `profiles.id`)
   - `loan_id`: uuid (references `loans.id`)
   - `amount`: numeric / bigint
   - `days_late`: int
   - `status`: enum (`unpaid`, `pending_verification`, `paid`)
   - `receipt_url`: text (Storage bucket path for QRIS payment proof)
   - `verified_at`: timestamptz
   - `verified_by`: uuid (references `profiles.id`)

6. **`repository_items`** (Koleksi Skripsi & Jurnal)
   - `id`: uuid
   - `title`: text
   - `author_id`: uuid (references `profiles.id`)
   - `prodi`: text
   - `year`: int
   - `abstract`: text
   - `pdf_url`: text
   - `status`: enum (`pending_curation`, `published`, `revision_needed`)
   - `reviewer_notes`: text

---

## 5. Next Steps for Developer / AI

1. Install Supabase client:
   ```bash
   npm install @supabase/supabase-js
   ```
2. Create `src/lib/supabase.ts` to initialize the client using environment variables:
   ```ts
   // VITE_SUPABASE_URL and VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY
   ```
3. Implement Authentication context / Zustand store to manage active session and user role (`student` vs `librarian`).
4. Replace local `useState` in `StudentPortal.tsx` and `LibrarianPortal.tsx` with hooks connecting to Supabase tables.
5. Create Supabase Storage buckets for:
   - `repository-files` (PDF thesis/journals)
   - `qris-receipts` (Payment slips uploaded by students)

---

## 6. Quick Commands

```bash
# Check TypeScript types
npx tsc --noEmit

# Run Vite dev server
npm run dev

# Create production build
npm run build
```

import { createClient } from "@supabase/supabase-js";
import { existsSync, readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { resolve } from "node:path";

const enabled = process.env.DEMO_SEED_ENABLED === "true";
if (!enabled) throw new Error("Set DEMO_SEED_ENABLED=true to run the demo seeder.");
if (process.env.NODE_ENV === "production") throw new Error("The demo seeder is disabled in production.");

const url = process.env.SUPABASE_URL ?? process.env.VITE_SUPABASE_URL;
const serviceKey = process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY;
if (!url || !serviceKey) {
  throw new Error("SUPABASE_URL and SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY are required.");
}

const supabase = createClient(url, serviceKey, { auth: { autoRefreshToken: false, persistSession: false } });
const emailFor = (nim) => `${nim}@siperpus.pnl.ac.id`;
const root = resolve(process.cwd());
const demoPdf = (name) => resolve(root, "public", "demo-pdfs", name);
const now = Date.now();
const iso = (offsetDays) => new Date(now + offsetDays * 86400000).toISOString();

  books: {

    overdue: "d1000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000003",

  ["2022573010041", "Mahasiswa Demo", "Teknologi Informasi dan Komputer"],

  ["2022573010049", "Muhammad Fajri", "Teknologi Informasi dan Komputer"],
].map(([nim, fullName, prodi]) => ({ nim, fullName, prodi, email: emailFor(nim) }));

const fail = (label, result) => {
  if (result.error) throw new Error(`${label}: ${result.error.message}`);
  return result.data;
};

const users = {};
const listedResult = await supabase.auth.admin.listUsers({ perPage: 1000 });
if (listedResult.error) throw new Error(`List Auth users: ${listedResult.error.message}`);
const listed = listedResult.data;
for (const student of students) {
  let authUser = listed?.users.find((user) => user.email === student.email);
  if (!authUser) {
    const result = await supabase.auth.admin.createUser({
      email: student.email,
      password: student.nim,
      email_confirm: true,
      user_metadata: { nim: student.nim, full_name: student.fullName, prodi: student.prodi },
      app_metadata: { role: "student" },
    });
    const created = fail(`Create ${student.nim}`, result);
    authUser = created.user;
    if (!authUser) throw new Error(`Create ${student.nim}: Auth user was not returned.`);
  }
  const appMetadata = { ...(authUser.app_metadata ?? {}), role: "student" };
  fail(`Student ${student.nim}`, await supabase.auth.admin.updateUserById(authUser.id, {
    password: student.nim,
    app_metadata: appMetadata,
  }));
  users[student.nim] = authUser.id;
  fail(`Profile ${student.nim}`, await supabase.from("profiles").upsert({
    id: authUser.id,
    nim: student.nim,
    full_name: student.fullName,
    role: "student",
    prodi: student.prodi,
    clear_status: true,
  }, { onConflict: "id" }));
}

  .limit(1)

const demoBookIds = books.map((book) => book.id);
const existingDemoLoans = await supabase.from("loans").select("id").in("book_id", demoBookIds);
if (existingDemoLoans.error) throw new Error(`Find demo loans: ${existingDemoLoans.error.message}`);
const existingDemoLoanIds = existingDemoLoans.data?.map((loan) => loan.id) ?? [];
if (existingDemoLoanIds.length) {
  fail("Reset demo fines", await supabase.from("fines").delete().in("loan_id", existingDemoLoanIds));
  fail("Reset demo loans", await supabase.from("loans").delete().in("id", existingDemoLoanIds));

fail("Reset demo holds", await supabase.from("holds").delete().in("book_id", demoBookIds));

const legacy = await supabase.from("books").select("id").eq("code", "INF-042").maybeSingle();
if (legacy.data?.id) {

FILE: src/App.tsx
================================================
import { useState } from "react";
import StudentPortal from "./StudentPortal";
import LibrarianPortal from "./LibrarianPortal";
import { Brand } from "./components/ui/Brand";
import { Button } from "./components/ui/Button";
import { Icon } from "./components/ui/Icon";
import { LoginModal } from "./components/ui/LoginModal";
import { ContactModal } from "./components/ui/ContactModal";
import { BooksView } from "./pages/CatalogPage";
import { RepositoryView } from "./pages/RepositoryPage";
import { AboutView } from "./pages/AboutPage";
import { Tab, ModalSource, RepoItem, Book } from "./types";
import { useAuth } from "./lib/authContext";
import { supabase } from "./lib/supabase";

export default function App() {
  const { user, profile, loading, signOut } = useAuth();
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<Tab>("books");
  const [modalSource, setModalSource] = useState<ModalSource | null>(null);
  const [showContactModal, setShowContactModal] = useState(false);
  const [mobileMenu, setMobileMenu] = useState(false);

  const tabs: { id: Tab; label: string }[] = [
    { id: "books", label: "Buku Fisik" },
    { id: "erepo", label: "E-Repository" },
    { id: "about", label: "Tentang" },
  ];

  const openAction = (source: ModalSource) => {
    if (!user) setModalSource(source);
  };
  const reserve = async (book: Book) => {
    if (!user) return openAction("reservation");
    const { error } = await supabase.rpc("reserve_book", { book_id: book.id });
    if (error) console.error(error);
  };
  const download = async (item: RepoItem) => {
    if (!user) return openAction("download");
    if (!item.pdfUrl) return;
    const { data, error } = await supabase.storage.from("repository-files").createSignedUrl(item.pdfUrl, 300);
    if (error || !data?.signedUrl) return;
    const downloadUrl = new URL(data.signedUrl);
    downloadUrl.searchParams.set("download", `${item.title.replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/gi, "-").replace(/^-|-$/g, "").toLowerCase()}.pdf`);
    const { error: recordError } = await supabase.rpc("record_download", { item_id: item.id });
    if (recordError) return;
    window.open(downloadUrl.toString(), "_blank", "noopener,noreferrer");
  };
  if (loading) return <div className="grid min-h-screen place-items-center bg-canvas text-muted">Memuat sesi...</div>;
  if (profile?.role === "librarian") return <LibrarianPortal profile={profile} onLogout={signOut} />;
  if (profile?.role === "student") return <StudentPortal profile={profile} onLogout={signOut} />;

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-canvas text-ink">
      <header className="sticky top-0 z-40 border-b border-emerald-950/10/80 bg-white/95 backdrop-blur-lg">
        <div className="mx-auto flex h-20 max-w-screen-2xl items-center justify-between px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
          <Brand />
          <nav className="hidden items-center gap-1 rounded-xl bg-canvas p-1 md:flex" aria-label="Navigasi utama">
            {tabs.map((tab) => (
              <button
                key={tab.id}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setActiveTab(tab.id)}
                className={`relative rounded-lg px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-bold transition ${
                  activeTab === tab.id ? "bg-white text-ink shadow-sm" : "text-muted hover:text-ink"
                }`}
              >
                {tab.label}
                {activeTab === tab.id && <span className="absolute inset-x-5 -bottom-1 h-0.5 rounded-full bg-accent" />}
              </button>
            ))}
          </nav>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            {user ? <Button variant="secondary" onClick={signOut} className="hidden sm:flex">Keluar</Button> : (
              <Button onClick={() => setModalSource("navbar")} className="hidden sm:flex">
                <Icon name="lock" size="size-4" /> Masuk SSO PNL
              </Button>
            )}
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setMobileMenu(!mobileMenu)}
              aria-label="Buka navigasi"
              className="flex size-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-emerald-950/10 text-muted md:hidden"
            >
              <Icon name={mobileMenu ? "close" : "list"} />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
        {mobileMenu && (
          <div className="border-t border-slate-100 bg-white p-4 md:hidden">
            <div className="grid gap-1">
              {tabs.map((tab) => (
                <button
                  key={tab.id}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    setActiveTab(tab.id);
                    setMobileMenu(false);
                  }}
                  className={`rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-left text-sm font-bold ${
                    activeTab === tab.id ? "bg-accent-soft text-ink" : "text-muted"
                  }`}
                >
                  {tab.label}
                </button>
              ))}
              {!user && (
                <Button
                  onClick={() => {
                    setModalSource("navbar");
                    setMobileMenu(false);
                  }}
                  className="mt-2"
                >
                  <Icon name="lock" size="size-4" /> Masuk SSO PNL
                </Button>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </header>

      <main className="mx-auto max-w-screen-2xl px-4 py-6 sm:px-6 md:py-8 lg:px-8">
        {activeTab === "books" && <BooksView onReserve={reserve} />}
        {activeTab === "erepo" && <RepositoryView onDownload={download} />}
        {activeTab === "about" && <AboutView onOpenContact={() => setShowContactModal(true)} />}
      </main>

      <footer className="mt-16 border-t border-emerald-950/10 bg-white">
        <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-screen-2xl flex-col gap-4 px-4 py-7 sm:px-6 md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between lg:px-8">
          <Brand />
          <p className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-400">© {new Date().getFullYear()} Politeknik Negeri Lhokseumawe · UPT Perpustakaan</p>
        </div>
      </footer>

      {modalSource && <LoginModal source={modalSource} onClose={() => setModalSource(null)} />}

      {showContactModal && <ContactModal onClose={() => setShowContactModal(false)} />}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/LibrarianPortal.tsx

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { BooksView } from "./pages/CatalogPage";
import { RepositoryView } from "./pages/RepositoryPage";
import { Catalog } from "./pages/LibrarianCatalog";
import { Curation } from "./pages/LibrarianCuration";
import { Fines } from "./pages/LibrarianFines";
import { Sirkulasi } from "./pages/LibrarianSirkulasi";
import { Dashboard } from "./pages/LibrarianDashboard";
import { useBooks } from "./lib/useSupabaseData";
import { supabase } from "./lib/supabase";
import { UserProfile } from "./lib/authContext";
import { View, Student, Hold, Loan, FineReq, Submission, fmtDate } from "./data/librarianData";
import { Icon, SideLink, ROW, TIP, NavItem } from "./pages/LibrarianShared";

type LookupPayload = {
  profile: { id: string; nim: string | null; full_name: string; prodi: string | null; clear_status: boolean };
  holds: Array<{ id: string; pin: string; expires_at: string; status: string; book: { title: string; code: string; shelf: string } }>;
  loans: Array<{ id: string; borrowed_at: string; due_date: string; returned_at: string | null; status: string; book: { title: string; code: string } }>;
  fines: Array<{ amount: number; status: string }>;
};

const errorMessage = (error: unknown) => error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Terjadi kesalahan saat memuat data.";

export default function LibrarianPortal({ profile, onLogout }: { profile: UserProfile; onLogout: () => Promise<void> }) {
  const [activeView, setActiveView] = useState<View>("dashboard");
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isCollapsed, setIsCollapsed] = useState(false);
  const [toast, setToast] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [lookup, setLookup] = useState("");
  const [student, setStudent] = useState<Student | null>(null);
  const [lookupLoading, setLookupLoading] = useState(false);
  const [lookupError, setLookupError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [fines, setFines] = useState<FineReq[]>([]);
  const [finesLoading, setFinesLoading] = useState(true);
  const [finesError, setFinesError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [subs, setSubs] = useState<Submission[]>([]);
  const [subsLoading, setSubsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [subsError, setSubsError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [metrics, setMetrics] = useState({ transactions: 0, holds: 0, fines: 0, submissions: 0 });
  const [metricsLoading, setMetricsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [metricsError, setMetricsError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const { books, setBooks, refetch: refetchBooks } = useBooks();
  const go = (view: View) => { setActiveView(view); setOpen(false); };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!toast) return;
    const timer = setTimeout(() => setToast(null), 3500);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [toast]);

  const loadFines = async () => {
    setFinesLoading(true);
    const { data, error } = await supabase.from("fines").select("id,amount,receipt_url,rejection_note,created_at,status,student:profiles!fines_user_id_fkey(nim,full_name,prodi)").eq("status", "pending_verification").order("created_at", { ascending: false });
    if (error) setFinesError(error.message);
    const rows = (data ?? []) as unknown as Array<{ id: string; amount: number; receipt_url: string | null; rejection_note: string | null; created_at: string; student: { nim: string | null; full_name: string; prodi: string | null } | null }>;
    setFines(rows.map((fine) => { const student = fine.student; return { id: fine.id, nim: student?.nim ?? "-", name: student?.full_name ?? "Mahasiswa", major: student?.prodi ?? "PNL", reason: "Keterlambatan pengembalian", amount: Number(fine.amount), uploaded: new Date(fine.created_at).toLocaleString("id-ID"), ref: fine.id, receiptUrl: fine.receipt_url, rejectionNote: fine.rejection_note }; }));
    setFinesLoading(false);
  };

  const loadSubs = async () => {
    setSubsLoading(true);
    const { data, error } = await supabase.from("repository_items").select("id,title,type,category,file_size,pdf_url,profiles(full_name,prodi)").eq("status", "pending_curation").order("created_at", { ascending: false });
    if (error) setSubsError(error.message);
    const rows = (data ?? []) as unknown as Array<{ id: string; title: string; type: string; category: "fiction" | "nonfiction"; file_size: string | null; pdf_url: string | null; profiles: Array<{ full_name: string; prodi: string | null }> | null }>;
    setSubs(rows.map((item) => { const author = item.profiles?.[0]; return { id: item.id, title: item.title, author: author?.full_name ?? "Mahasiswa", major: author?.prodi ?? "PNL", size: item.file_size ?? "Ukuran tidak tersedia", category: item.category === "fiction" ? "Fiksi" : "Non-Fiksi", type: item.type, pdfUrl: item.pdf_url }; }));
    setSubsLoading(false);
  };

  const loadMetrics = async () => {
    setMetricsLoading(true);
    const start = new Date();
    start.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
    const [transactions, holds, pendingFines, pendingSubs] = await Promise.all([
      supabase.from("loans").select("id,borrowed_at,returned_at").or(`borrowed_at.gte.${start.toISOString()},returned_at.gte.${start.toISOString()}`),
      supabase.from("holds").select("id", { count: "exact", head: true }).eq("status", "active"),
      supabase.from("fines").select("id", { count: "exact", head: true }).eq("status", "pending_verification"),
      supabase.from("repository_items").select("id", { count: "exact", head: true }).eq("status", "pending_curation"),
    ]);
    const firstError = transactions.error || holds.error || pendingFines.error || pendingSubs.error;
    if (firstError) setMetricsError(firstError.message);
    setMetrics({ transactions: new Set((transactions.data ?? []).map((row) => row.id)).size, holds: holds.count ?? 0, fines: pendingFines.count ?? 0, submissions: pendingSubs.count ?? 0 });
    setMetricsLoading(false);
  };

  useEffect(() => { void Promise.all([loadFines(), loadSubs(), loadMetrics()]); }, []);

  const searchStudent = async (identifier: string) => {
    setLookup(identifier);
    setLookupLoading(true);
    setLookupError(null);
    const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("lookup_librarian", { identifier: identifier.trim() });
    if (error) setLookupError(error.message);
    const payload = data as LookupPayload | null;
    if (!payload) setStudent(null);
    else {
      const activeHolds: Hold[] = payload.holds.filter((hold) => hold.status === "active").map((hold) => ({ id: hold.id, pin: hold.pin, title: hold.book.title, code: hold.book.code, shelf: hold.book.shelf, expires: fmtDate(new Date(hold.expires_at)), expires_at: hold.expires_at }));
      const loans: Loan[] = payload.loans.map((loan) => ({ id: loan.id, title: loan.book.title, code: loan.book.code, start: fmtDate(new Date(loan.borrowed_at)), due: fmtDate(new Date(loan.due_date)), daysLeft: Math.ceil((Date.parse(loan.due_date) - Date.now()) / 86400000), returned_at: loan.returned_at }));
      setStudent({ id: payload.profile.id, nim: payload.profile.nim ?? "-", name: payload.profile.full_name, major: payload.profile.prodi ?? "PNL", holds: activeHolds, loans, history: payload.loans.filter((loan) => loan.returned_at).map((loan) => ({ date: fmtDate(new Date(loan.returned_at!)), action: "Pengembalian", title: loan.book.title })), fine: payload.fines.filter((fine) => fine.status !== "paid").reduce((sum, fine) => sum + Number(fine.amount), 0), clear: payload.profile.clear_status });
    }
    setLookupLoading(false);
  };

  const handover = async (holdId: string) => {
    const { error } = await supabase.rpc("handover_hold", { hold_id: holdId });
    setToast(error ? error.message : "Buku berhasil diserahkan.");
    if (!error) { await searchStudent(lookup); await loadMetrics(); }
  };
  const returnLoan = async (loanId: string) => {
    const { error } = await supabase.rpc("return_loan", { loan_id: loanId });
    setToast(error ? error.message : "Pengembalian berhasil dicatat.");
    if (!error) { await searchStudent(lookup); await Promise.all([loadMetrics(), loadFines()]); }
  };
  const approveFine = async (fine: FineReq) => {
    const { error } = await supabase.rpc("approve_fine", { fine_id: fine.id });
    setToast(error ? error.message : "Denda disetujui.");
    if (!error) await Promise.all([loadFines(), loadMetrics()]);
  };
  const rejectFine = async (fine: FineReq, note: string) => {
    const { error } = await supabase.rpc("reject_fine", { fine_id: fine.id, note });
    setToast(error ? error.message : "Bukti pembayaran ditolak.");
    if (!error) await loadFines();
  };
  const publish = async (submission: Submission) => {
    const { error } = await supabase.from("repository_items").update({ status: "published", reviewer_notes: null }).eq("id", submission.id);
    setToast(error ? error.message : "Karya dipublikasikan.");
    if (!error) await Promise.all([loadSubs(), loadMetrics()]);
  };
  const revise = async (submission: Submission, note: string) => {
    const { error } = await supabase.from("repository_items").update({ status: "revision_needed", reviewer_notes: note }).eq("id", submission.id);
    setToast(error ? error.message : "Permintaan revisi dikirim.");
    if (!error) await Promise.all([loadSubs(), loadMetrics()]);
  };

  const nav: NavItem[] = [
    { key: "dashboard", label: "Dashboard Operasional", icon: "grid" },
    { key: "sirkulasi", label: "Loket & Lookup NIM", icon: "userCheck", count: metrics.holds },
    { key: "fines", label: "Verifikasi Denda QRIS", icon: "receipt", count: metrics.fines },
    { key: "erepo_curation", label: "Kurasi E-Repository", icon: "fileCheck", count: metrics.submissions },
    { key: "catalog", label: "Kelola Katalog & Stok", icon: "books" },
  ];

  return <div className="min-h-screen bg-canvas text-ink">
    {open && <div className="fixed inset-0 z-30 bg-forest/50 lg:hidden" onClick={() => setOpen(false)} />}
    <aside className={`fixed inset-y-0 left-0 z-40 flex w-72 flex-col bg-forest p-4 text-white transition-[transform,width] duration-200 lg:translate-x-0 ${isCollapsed ? "lg:w-20" : "lg:w-72"} ${open ? "translate-x-0" : "-translate-x-full"}`}>
      <div className={`flex items-center justify-between px-1 ${isCollapsed ? "lg:justify-center lg:px-0" : ""}`}><div className="flex items-center gap-3"><div className="grid size-10 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-xl bg-accent font-black text-forest">SA</div><div className={isCollapsed ? "lg:hidden" : ""}><p className="font-extrabold tracking-tight">SIPERPUS Admin</p><p className="text-[11px] text-white/50">Politeknik Negeri Lhokseumawe</p></div></div><button onClick={() => setOpen(false)} className="lg:hidden" aria-label="Tutup menu"><Icon d="x" /></button></div>
      <span className={`mx-1 mt-5 self-start rounded-full border border-accent/40 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-accent ${isCollapsed ? "lg:hidden" : ""}`}>Pustakawan PNL</span>
      <nav aria-label="Navigasi admin" className="mt-6 space-y-1">{nav.map((item) => <SideLink key={item.key} item={item} active={activeView === item.key} collapsed={isCollapsed} onClick={() => go(item.key)} />)}</nav>
      <button onClick={() => void onLogout()} aria-label="Keluar Admin" className={`${ROW} mt-auto text-white/80 hover:bg-danger/20 hover:text-white ${isCollapsed ? "lg:justify-center lg:px-0" : ""}`}><Icon d="logout" className="size-5 shrink-0" /><span className={`truncate ${isCollapsed ? "lg:hidden" : ""}`}>Keluar Admin</span>{isCollapsed && <span role="tooltip" className={TIP}>Keluar Admin</span>}</button>
      <button onClick={() => setIsCollapsed((value) => !value)} aria-label={isCollapsed ? "Perluas sidebar" : "Ciutkan sidebar"} aria-expanded={!isCollapsed} className="absolute -right-3 top-[34px] hidden size-6 place-items-center rounded-full border border-white/15 bg-forest-light text-white shadow-md lg:grid"><Icon d={isCollapsed ? "chevR" : "chevL"} className="size-4" /></button>
    </aside>
    <div className={`transition-[padding] duration-200 ${isCollapsed ? "lg:pl-20" : "lg:pl-72"}`}>
      <header className="sticky top-0 z-20 flex items-center gap-3 border-b border-emerald-950/10 bg-canvas/85 px-4 py-3 backdrop-blur md:px-8"><button onClick={() => setOpen(true)} className="rounded-lg p-2 hover:bg-white lg:hidden" aria-label="Buka menu"><Icon d="menu" /></button><div className="flex items-center gap-3"><div className="grid size-10 place-items-center rounded-full bg-forest text-sm font-bold text-accent">{profile.full_name.split(" ").map((word) => word[0]).slice(0, 2).join("").toUpperCase()}</div><div className="hidden leading-tight md:block"><p className="text-sm font-bold">{profile.full_name}</p><p className="text-xs text-muted">{profile.nim ?? "Pustakawan"} · {profile.prodi ?? "UPT Perpustakaan"}</p></div></div></header>
      <main className="mx-auto max-w-screen-2xl p-4 md:p-8">
        {activeView === "dashboard" && <Dashboard go={go} loading={metricsLoading} error={metricsError} metrics={metrics} />}
        {activeView === "sirkulasi" && <Sirkulasi student={student} query={lookup} setQuery={setLookup} loading={lookupLoading} error={lookupError} onSearch={searchStudent} onHandover={handover} onReturn={returnLoan} />}
        {activeView === "fines" && <Fines fines={fines} loading={finesLoading} error={finesError} onApprove={approveFine} onReject={rejectFine} />}
        {activeView === "erepo_curation" && <Curation subs={subs} loading={subsLoading} error={subsError} onPublish={publish} onRevise={revise} />}
        {activeView === "catalog" && <Catalog items={books} setItems={setBooks} toast={setToast} refetch={refetchBooks} />}
      </main>
    </div>
    {toast && <div role="status" className="fixed bottom-6 left-1/2 z-50 flex max-w-[90vw] -translate-x-1/2 items-center gap-3 rounded-xl bg-forest px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white shadow-2xl"><span className="grid size-6 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full bg-accent text-forest"><Icon d="check" className="size-4" /></span>{toast}</div>}
  </div>;
}

import { useEffect, useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { BooksView } from "./pages/CatalogPage";
import { RepositoryView } from "./pages/RepositoryPage";
import { View } from "./data/portalData";
import { StudentDashboard } from "./pages/StudentDashboard";
import { P } from "./pages/StudentShared";
import { ERepo } from "./pages/StudentERepo";
import { Circulation } from "./pages/StudentCirculation";
import { Fines } from "./pages/StudentFines";
import { QrisMock } from "./pages/QrisMock";
import { Icon, Badge, Modal, UploadModal, QrisModal, Cover, Title, Stat, useCountdown, btnPrimary, btnSecondary, btnGhost, card, FINE_RATE, rupiah } from "./pages/StudentShared";
import { UserProfile } from "./lib/authContext";
import { Book, RepoItem } from "./types";
import { supabase } from "./lib/supabase";



/* ---------- Icons ---------- */

/* ---------- Data ---------- */
const books: { t: string; a: string; y: number; k: string; s: number; c: string }[] = [];
const papers: { t: string; a: string; y: number; j: string; dl: number }[] = [];

/* ---------- UI atoms ---------- */


/* ---------- Fines ---------- */
type FineState = "unpaid" | "pending_verification" | "rejected";
type Fine = { id: string; b: (typeof books)[number]; from: string; due: string; returned: string; late: number; state: FineState; file?: string };








/* ---------- Shell ---------- */
type NavItem = { key: View; label: string; icon: keyof typeof P };

const TIP = "pointer-events-none invisible absolute left-full z-50 ml-3 hidden whitespace-nowrap rounded-md bg-slate-900 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs text-white opacity-0 shadow-lg transition-opacity duration-150 group-hover:visible group-hover:opacity-100 group-focus-visible:visible group-focus-visible:opacity-100 lg:block";
const ROW = "group relative flex min-h-[42px] w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-xl px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium tracking-[-0.01em] transition-[background-color,color] duration-150 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-accent";

function SideLink({ item, active, collapsed, onClick }: { item: NavItem; active: boolean; collapsed: boolean; onClick: () => void }) {
  return (
    <button
      onClick={onClick}
      aria-label={item.label}
      aria-current={active ? "page" : undefined}
      className={`${ROW} ${collapsed ? "lg:justify-center lg:px-0" : ""} ${active ? "bg-accent font-semibold text-forest" : "text-white/70 hover:bg-white/10 hover:text-white"}`}
    >
      <Icon d={item.icon} className="size-5 shrink-0" />
      <span className={`truncate text-left ${collapsed ? "lg:hidden" : ""}`}>{item.label}</span>
      {collapsed && <span role="tooltip" className={TIP}>{item.label}</span>}
    </button>
  );
}





export default function StudentPortal({ profile, onLogout }: { profile: UserProfile; onLogout: () => Promise<void> }) {
  const [activeView, setActiveView] = useState<View>("dashboard");
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isCollapsed, setIsCollapsed] = useState(false);
  const [toast, setToast] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const go = (v: View) => { setActiveView(v); setOpen(false); };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!toast) return;
    const t = setTimeout(() => setToast(null), 3500);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, [toast]);

  const handleStudentReserve = async (book: Book) => {
    const { error } = await supabase.rpc("reserve_book", { book_id: book.id });
    setToast(error ? error.message : "Reservasi berhasil. Tiket hold 3 hari tersedia di Dashboard Utama.");
  };
  const handleStudentDownload = async (item: RepoItem) => {
    if (!item.pdfUrl) return setToast("Berkas PDF belum tersedia.");
    const { data, error } = await supabase.storage.from("repository-files").createSignedUrl(item.pdfUrl, 300);
    if (error || !data?.signedUrl) return setToast(error?.message ?? "Berkas tidak dapat diakses.");
    const downloadUrl = new URL(data.signedUrl);
    downloadUrl.searchParams.set("download", `${item.title.replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/gi, "-").replace(/^-|-$/g, "").toLowerCase()}.pdf`);
    const { error: recordError } = await supabase.rpc("record_download", { item_id: item.id });
    if (recordError) return setToast(recordError.message);
    window.open(downloadUrl.toString(), "_blank", "noopener,noreferrer");
  };

  const dashboard: NavItem = { key: "dashboard", label: "Dashboard", icon: "grid" };
  const catalog: NavItem[] = [
    { key: "catalog_books", label: "Buku Fisik", icon: "books" },
    { key: "catalog_erepo", label: "E-Repository", icon: "layers" },
  ];
  const others: NavItem[] = [
    { key: "circulation", label: "Sirkulasi", icon: "clock" },
    { key: "erepo_student", label: "Unggah Karya", icon: "file" },
  ];
  const link = (item: NavItem) => (
    <SideLink key={item.key} item={item} active={activeView === item.key} collapsed={isCollapsed} onClick={() => go(item.key)} />
  );

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-canvas text-ink">
      {open && <div className="fixed inset-0 z-30 bg-forest/50 lg:hidden" onClick={() => setOpen(false)} />}
      <aside className={`fixed inset-y-0 left-0 z-40 flex w-72 flex-col bg-forest p-4 text-white transition-[transform,width] duration-200 lg:translate-x-0 ${isCollapsed ? "lg:w-20" : "lg:w-72"} ${open ? "translate-x-0" : "-translate-x-full"}`}>
        <div className={`flex items-center justify-between px-1 ${isCollapsed ? "lg:justify-center lg:px-0" : ""}`}>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="grid size-10 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-xl bg-accent font-black text-forest">SR</div>
            <div className={isCollapsed ? "lg:hidden" : ""}><p className="font-extrabold tracking-tight">SIPERPUS-REPO</p><p className="text-[11px] text-white/50">Politeknik Negeri Lhokseumawe</p></div>
          </div>
          <button onClick={() => setOpen(false)} className="lg:hidden" aria-label="Tutup menu"><Icon d="x" /></button>
        </div>
        <span className={`mx-1 mt-5 self-start rounded-full border border-accent/40 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-accent ${isCollapsed ? "lg:hidden" : ""}`}>Mahasiswa PNL</span>
        <nav aria-label="Navigasi utama" className="mt-6 space-y-1">
          {link(dashboard)}
          <p className={`my-3 px-3 pt-2 text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.15em] text-white/50 ${isCollapsed ? "lg:hidden" : ""}`}>Jelajahi Katalog</p>
          {isCollapsed && <div className="mx-3 my-3 hidden border-t border-white/15 lg:block" />}
          <div className={`space-y-1 ${isCollapsed ? "" : "lg:ml-5 lg:border-l lg:border-white/10 lg:pl-2"}`}>{catalog.map(link)}</div>
          {isCollapsed && <div className="mx-3 my-3 hidden border-t border-white/15 lg:block" />}
          <div className="space-y-1 pt-1">{others.map(link)}</div>
        </nav>
        <div className={`mt-auto rounded-xl bg-white/5 p-4 text-xs text-white/60 ${isCollapsed ? "lg:hidden" : ""}`}>Jam layanan: Senin–Jumat, 08.00–16.30 WIB</div>
        <button onClick={onLogout} aria-label="Keluar SSO" className={`${ROW} mt-3 text-white/80 hover:bg-danger/20 hover:text-white ${isCollapsed ? "lg:justify-center lg:px-0" : ""}`}>
          <Icon d="logout" className="size-5 shrink-0" />
          <span className={`truncate ${isCollapsed ? "lg:hidden" : ""}`}>Keluar SSO</span>
          {isCollapsed && <span role="tooltip" className={TIP}>Keluar SSO</span>}
        </button>
        <button
          onClick={() => setIsCollapsed((c) => !c)}
          aria-label={isCollapsed ? "Perluas sidebar" : "Ciutkan sidebar"}
          aria-expanded={!isCollapsed}
          className="absolute -right-3 top-[34px] hidden size-6 place-items-center rounded-full border border-white/15 bg-forest-light text-white shadow-md transition-[background-color] duration-150 hover:bg-forest-light/80 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-accent lg:grid"
        >
          <Icon d={isCollapsed ? "chevR" : "chevL"} className="size-4" />
        </button>
      </aside>

      <div className={`transition-[padding] duration-200 ${isCollapsed ? "lg:pl-20" : "lg:pl-72"}`}>
        <header className="sticky top-0 z-20 flex items-center gap-3 border-b border-emerald-950/10 bg-canvas/85 px-4 py-3 backdrop-blur md:px-8">
          <button onClick={() => setOpen(true)} className="rounded-lg p-2 hover:bg-white lg:hidden" aria-label="Buka menu"><Icon d="menu" /></button>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="grid size-10 place-items-center rounded-full bg-forest text-sm font-bold text-accent">{(profile.full_name || "?").split(" ").map((word) => word[0]).slice(0, 2).join("").toUpperCase()}</div>
            <div className="leading-tight"><p className="text-sm font-bold">Halo, {profile.full_name}</p><p className="text-xs text-muted">{profile.nim ?? "Mahasiswa"} · {profile.prodi ?? "PNL"}</p></div>
          </div>
          <button className="relative ml-auto rounded-xl border border-emerald-950/10 bg-white p-2.5" aria-label="Notifikasi">
            <Icon d="bell" className="size-5" /><span className="absolute right-2 top-2 size-2 rounded-full bg-danger ring-2 ring-white" />
          </button>
        </header>
        <main className="mx-auto max-w-screen-2xl p-4 md:p-8">
          {activeView === "dashboard" && <StudentDashboard go={go} />}
          {activeView === "catalog_books" && <BooksView onReserve={handleStudentReserve} />}
          {activeView === "catalog_erepo" && <RepositoryView onDownload={handleStudentDownload} />}
          {activeView === "circulation" && <Circulation />}
          {activeView === "erepo_student" && <ERepo />}
        </main>
      </div>

      {toast && (
        <div role="status" className="fixed bottom-6 left-1/2 z-50 flex max-w-[90vw] -translate-x-1/2 items-center gap-3 rounded-xl bg-forest px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white shadow-2xl">
          <span className="grid size-6 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full bg-accent text-forest"><Icon d="check" className="size-4" /></span>
          {toast}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/components/ui/Availability.tsx

import { Icon } from "./Icon";
import { Button } from "./Button";

export function Availability({ stock }: { stock: number }) {
  return stock > 0 ? (
    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-success-soft px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-bold text-success">
      <span className="size-1.5 rounded-full bg-success" /> Tersedia
    </span>
  ) : (
    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-danger-soft px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-bold text-danger">
      <span className="size-1.5 rounded-full bg-danger" /> Dipinjam
    </span>
  );
}

export function ManageButton({ onClick, className = "" }: { onClick: () => void; className?: string }) {
  return (
    <Button variant="secondary" onClick={onClick} className={className}>
      <Icon name="layers" size="size-4" /> Edit / Kelola
    </Button>
  );
}

FILE: src/components/ui/BookCard.tsx

import { Icon } from "./Icon";
import { Button } from "./Button";
import { BookCover } from "./BookCover";
import { Availability } from "./Availability";
import { ManageButton } from "./ManageButton";
import { Layout, Book } from "../../types";

export function BookCard({ book, layout, onReserve, onManage }: { book: Book; layout: Layout; onReserve: (book: Book) => void; onManage?: (book: Book) => void }) {
  if (layout === "list") {
    return (
      <article className="group flex flex-col gap-5 rounded-2xl border border-emerald-950/10 bg-white p-4 transition hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:border-forest/20 hover:shadow-lg hover:shadow-emerald-950/10 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
        <BookCover book={book} compact />
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <div className="mb-2 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
            <span className="rounded-full bg-accent-soft px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-bold text-forest">{book.shelf}</span>
            <Availability stock={book.stock} />
          </div>
          <h3 className="text-lg font-extrabold leading-snug text-ink">{book.title}</h3>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted">{book.author}</p>
          <div className="mt-3 flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-semibold text-muted">
            <Icon name="books" size="size-4" /> Stok fisik: <strong className="text-ink">{book.stock} eksemplar</strong>
          </div>
        </div>
        {onManage ? <ManageButton onClick={() => onManage(book)} className="w-full sm:w-auto" /> : (
        <Button onClick={() => onReserve(book)} disabled={book.stock === 0} className="w-full sm:w-auto">
          <Icon name="calendar" size="size-4" /> Reservasi <span className="hidden lg:inline">(Hold 3 Hari)</span>
        </Button>
        )}
      </article>
    );
  }
  const dense = layout === "grid5" || layout === "grid6";
  return (
    <article className={`group flex h-full flex-col rounded-2xl border border-emerald-950/10 bg-white transition hover:-translate-y-1 hover:border-forest/25 hover:shadow-xl hover:shadow-emerald-950/10 ${dense ? "p-2.5" : "p-4"}`}>
      <BookCover book={book} dense={dense} />
      <div className={`flex flex-1 flex-col ${dense ? "pt-3" : "pt-5"}`}>
        <div className={`flex flex-wrap items-center ${dense ? "mb-2 gap-1" : "mb-3 gap-2"}`}>
          <span className={`rounded-full bg-accent-soft text-xs font-bold text-forest ${dense ? "px-2 py-0.5" : "px-2.5 py-1"}`}>{book.shelf}</span>
          <Availability stock={book.stock} />
        </div>
        <h3 className={`${dense ? "text-sm" : "text-base"} font-extrabold leading-snug text-ink`}>{book.title}</h3>
        <p className={`mt-1 text-muted ${dense ? "text-xs" : "text-sm"}`}>{book.author}</p>
        <div className={`${dense ? "mb-3 mt-3 pt-2" : "mb-4 mt-4 pt-3"} flex items-center gap-2 border-t border-slate-100 text-xs font-semibold text-muted`}>
          <Icon name="books" size="size-4" /> Stok: <strong className="text-ink">{book.stock} eksemplar</strong>
        </div>
        {onManage ? <ManageButton onClick={() => onManage(book)} className={`mt-auto w-full ${dense ? "px-2 py-2 text-xs" : ""}`} /> : (
        <Button onClick={() => onReserve(book)} disabled={book.stock === 0} className={`mt-auto w-full ${dense ? "px-2 py-2 text-xs" : ""}`}>
          <Icon name="calendar" size="size-4" /> {dense ? "Reservasi" : "Reservasi (Hold 3 Hari)"}
        </Button>
        )}
      </div>
    </article>
  );
}

FILE: src/components/ui/BookCover.tsx
================================================
import { Icon } from "./Icon";
import { Book } from "../../types";

export function BookCover({ book, compact = false, dense = false }: { book: Book; compact?: boolean; dense?: boolean }) {
  if (book.cover.startsWith("http")) {
    return <img src={book.cover} alt={`Sampul ${book.title}`} className={`relative shrink-0 rounded-xl object-cover shadow-lg ${compact ? "h-32 w-24" : "aspect-[3/4] w-full"}`} />;
  }
  return (
    <div className={`relative shrink-0 overflow-hidden rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br ${book.cover || "from-forest to-forest-light"} text-white shadow-lg ${compact ? "h-32 w-24" : "aspect-[3/4] w-full"}`}>
      <div className="absolute inset-y-0 left-0 w-2 bg-white/10" />
      <div className={`flex h-full flex-col justify-between ${dense ? "p-2.5" : "p-4"}`}>
        <div className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-accent">PNL Library</div>
        <Icon name="book" size={compact ? "size-7" : dense ? "size-7" : "size-10"} />
        <div>
          <div className={`h-0.5 bg-accent ${dense ? "w-5" : "w-8"}`} />
          <div className={`${dense ? "mt-1" : "mt-2"} text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-white/70`}>{book.code}</div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

================================================
FILE: src/components/ui/Button.tsx
================================================
import { type ReactNode } from "react";

export function Button({
  children,
  onClick,
  variant = "primary",
  className = "",
  disabled = false,
  type = "button",
}: {
  children: ReactNode;
  onClick?: () => void;
  variant?: "primary" | "secondary" | "ghost" | "dark";
  className?: string;
  disabled?: boolean;
  type?: "button" | "submit";
}) {
  const styles = {
    primary: "bg-accent text-ink hover:bg-accent-hover shadow-sm",
    secondary: "border border-emerald-950/10 bg-white text-muted hover:border-forest/20 hover:bg-canvas",
    ghost: "text-muted hover:bg-canvas hover:text-ink",
    dark: "bg-forest text-white hover:bg-forest-light",
  };
  return (
    <button type={type} onClick={onClick} disabled={disabled} className={`inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-bold transition-all focus:outline-none focus:ring-4 focus:ring-accent/30 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-45 ${styles[variant]} ${className}`}>
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}

import { useState } from "react";
import { Icon } from "./Icon";
import { Button } from "./Button";

export function ContactModal({ onClose }: { onClose: () => void }) {
  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false);
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({ name: "", email: "", subject: "", message: "" });

  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setSubmitted(true);
  };

  return (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-end justify-center bg-forest/60 p-0 backdrop-blur-sm sm:items-center sm:p-6"
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
      aria-labelledby="contact-title"
      onMouseDown={(e) => {
        if (e.target === e.currentTarget) onClose();
      }}
    >
      {/* Modal Container: Diperkecil ke max-w-sm tanpa clutter */}
      <div className="flex w-full flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-t-2xl bg-white shadow-2xl sm:max-w-sm sm:rounded-2xl">
        {/* Clean Header: Langsung gabung Judul & Subtitle */}
        <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-100 px-5 py-3.5">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <div className="flex size-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-accent text-ink">
              <Icon name="mail" size="size-4" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h2 id="contact-title" className="text-sm font-bold text-ink">
                Hubungi Pustakawan
              </h2>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-muted">Tanyakan seputar buku & e-repository</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            aria-label="Tutup modal"
            className="flex size-7 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-canvas text-muted transition hover:bg-slate-200 hover:text-ink"
          >
            <Icon name="close" size="size-4" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Form Body langsung tanpa kotak email/telepon */}
        <div className="p-5">
          {submitted ? (
            <div className="py-6 text-center">
              <div className="mx-auto mb-3 flex size-12 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-accent text-ink">
                <Icon name="check" size="size-6" />
              </div>
              <h3 className="text-base font-extrabold text-ink">Pesan Terkirim!</h3>
              <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted">
                Pustakawan akan membalas via email dalam 1x24 jam kerja.
              </p>
              <Button onClick={onClose} className="mt-5 h-9 w-full text-xs">
                Selesai
              </Button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-3">
              <div>
                <label htmlFor="contact-name" className="text-xs font-bold text-muted">
                  Nama Lengkap
                </label>
                <input
                  id="contact-name"
                  required
                  value={formData.name}
                  onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, name: e.target.value })}
                  placeholder="Nama Anda"
                  className="mt-1 h-9 w-full rounded-lg border border-emerald-950/10 px-3 text-xs font-medium outline-none placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:border-accent focus:ring-2 focus:ring-accent/20"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label htmlFor="contact-email" className="text-xs font-bold text-muted">
                  Email
                </label>
                <input
                  id="contact-email"
                  type="email"
                  required
                  value={formData.email}
                  onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, email: e.target.value })}
                  placeholder="contoh@pnl.ac.id"
                  className="mt-1 h-9 w-full rounded-lg border border-emerald-950/10 px-3 text-xs font-medium outline-none placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:border-accent focus:ring-2 focus:ring-accent/20"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label htmlFor="contact-subject" className="text-xs font-bold text-muted">
                  Topik Pertanyaan
                </label>
                <select
                  id="contact-subject"
                  required
                  value={formData.subject}
                  onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, subject: e.target.value })}
                  className="mt-1 h-9 w-full rounded-lg border border-emerald-950/10 bg-white px-3 text-xs font-medium outline-none focus:border-accent focus:ring-2 focus:ring-accent/20"
                >
                  <option value="">Pilih topik...</option>
                  <option value="sirkulasi">Sirkulasi & Peminjaman Buku</option>
                  <option value="erepo">E-Repository & Karya Ilmiah</option>
                  <option value="denda">Informasi Denda / Bebas Pustaka</option>
                  <option value="lainnya">Pertanyaan Lainnya</option>
                </select>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label htmlFor="contact-message" className="text-xs font-bold text-muted">
                  Isi Pesan
                </label>
                <textarea
                  id="contact-message"
                  required
                  rows={2.5}
                  value={formData.message}
                  onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, message: e.target.value })}
                  placeholder="Detail pertanyaan Anda..."
                  className="mt-1 w-full rounded-lg border border-emerald-950/10 p-2.5 text-xs font-medium outline-none placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:border-accent focus:ring-2 focus:ring-accent/20"
                />
              </div>

              <Button type="submit" className="mt-2 h-9 w-full text-xs">
                <Icon name="mail" size="size-3.5" /> Kirim Pesan
              </Button>
            </form>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/components/ui/LayoutSwitcher.tsx

import { Icon } from "./Icon";
import { Layout, IconName } from "../../types";

export function LayoutSwitcher({ value, onChange }: { value: Layout; onChange: (layout: Layout) => void }) {
  const layouts: { id: Layout; icon: IconName; label: string }[] = [
    { id: "list", icon: "list", label: "Daftar" },
    { id: "grid4", icon: "grid4", label: "Grid 4" },
    { id: "grid5", icon: "grid5", label: "Grid 5" },
    { id: "grid6", icon: "grid6", label: "Grid 6" },
  ];
  return (
    <div className="flex rounded-xl border border-emerald-950/10 bg-canvas p-1" aria-label="Pilih tampilan">
      {layouts.map((layout) => (
        <button
          key={layout.id}
          type="button"
          title={layout.label}
          aria-label={layout.label}
          onClick={() => onChange(layout.id)}
          className={`flex size-9 items-center justify-center rounded-lg transition ${value === layout.id ? "bg-forest text-accent shadow-sm" : "text-slate-400 hover:bg-white hover:text-forest"}`}
        >
          <Icon name={layout.icon} size="size-4" />
        </button>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}



================================================
FILE: src/components/ui/LoginModal.tsx
================================================
import { useState } from "react";
import { Icon } from "./Icon";
import { Button } from "./Button";
import { Brand } from "./Brand";
import { ModalSource } from "../../types";
import { supabase } from "../../lib/supabase";

export function LoginModal({ source, onClose }: { source: ModalSource; onClose: () => void }) {
  const actionTriggered = source !== "navbar";
  const [identity, setIdentity] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-end justify-center bg-forest/60 p-0 backdrop-blur-sm sm:items-center sm:p-6" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="login-title" onMouseDown={(event) => { if (event.target === event.currentTarget) onClose(); }}>
      <div className="w-full overflow-hidden rounded-t-3xl bg-white shadow-2xl sm:max-w-md sm:rounded-3xl">
        <div className="flex items-start justify-between border-b border-slate-100 px-6 py-5">
          <Brand />
          <button type="button" onClick={onClose} aria-label="Tutup modal" className="flex size-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-canvas text-muted transition hover:bg-slate-200 hover:text-ink"><Icon name="close" size="size-4" /></button>
        </div>
        <div className="p-6">
          <div className="mb-5">
            <div className="mb-3 flex size-11 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-accent text-ink"><Icon name="lock" /></div>
            <h2 id="login-title" className="text-2xl font-black tracking-tight text-ink">Masuk dengan SSO PNL</h2>
            <p className="mt-1.5 text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted">Gunakan akun institusi Politeknik Negeri Lhokseumawe.</p>
          </div>
          {actionTriggered && (
            <div className="mb-5 flex gap-3 rounded-xl border border-accent/40 bg-accent-soft p-3.5 text-sm font-semibold leading-relaxed text-muted">
              <span className="mt-0.5 shrink-0 text-forest"><Icon name="info" size="size-5" /></span>
              {source === "download" ? "Login SSO PNL diperlukan untuk mengunduh berkas PDF mahasiswa." : "Fitur reservasi & download PDF khusus untuk Mahasiswa PNL."}
            </div>
          )}
          <form onSubmit={async (event) => {
            event.preventDefault();
            setLoading(true);
            setError(null);
            const normalizedIdentity = identity.replace(/\s+/g, "").toLowerCase();
            const email = `${normalizedIdentity}@siperpus.pnl.ac.id`;
            const { error: signInError } = await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({ email, password });
            if (signInError) setError("NIM/NIP atau password salah.");
            else onClose();
            setLoading(false);
          }}>
            <label className="text-sm font-bold text-muted" htmlFor="identity">NIM / NIP</label>
            <div className="relative mt-2">
              <span className="absolute left-4 top-3.5 text-slate-400"><Icon name="user" /></span>
              <input id="identity" required value={identity} onChange={(event) => setIdentity(event.target.value)} placeholder="Masukkan NIM atau NIP" className="h-12 w-full rounded-xl border border-emerald-950/10 pl-12 pr-4 text-sm font-medium outline-none placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:border-accent focus:ring-4 focus:ring-accent/15" />
            </div>
            <input id="password" required type="password" value={password} onChange={(event) => setPassword(event.target.value)} placeholder="Password" className="mt-3 h-12 w-full rounded-xl border border-emerald-950/10 px-4 text-sm font-medium outline-none placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:border-accent focus:ring-4 focus:ring-accent/15" />
            {error && <p role="alert" className="mt-3 text-sm font-semibold text-danger">{error}</p>}
            <Button type="submit" disabled={loading} className="mt-4 w-full"><Icon name="shield" size="size-4" /> {loading ? "Memproses..." : "Masuk"}</Button>
          </form>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { Icon } from "./Icon";
import { Button } from "./Button";
import { Layout, RepoItem } from "../../types";

export function RepoCard({ item, layout, onDownload }: { item: RepoItem; layout: Layout; onDownload: (item: RepoItem) => void }) {
  const list = layout === "list";
  const dense = layout === "grid5" || layout === "grid6";
  return (
    <article className={`group flex rounded-2xl border border-emerald-950/10 bg-white transition hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:border-forest/25 hover:shadow-lg hover:shadow-emerald-950/10 ${dense ? "p-3" : "p-5"} ${list ? "flex-col gap-5 sm:flex-row sm:items-center" : "h-full flex-col"}`}>
      <div className={`flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-forest text-accent ${list ? "size-20" : dense ? "mb-3 size-10" : "mb-5 size-14"}`}>
        <Icon name="file" size={list ? "size-8" : dense ? "size-5" : "size-7"} />
      </div>
      <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
        <div className="mb-3 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          <span className={`rounded-full px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-bold ${item.accent}`}>{item.type}</span>
          <span className="rounded-full bg-canvas px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-bold text-muted">{item.department}</span>
        </div>
        <h3 className={`${dense ? "text-sm" : "text-lg"} font-extrabold leading-snug text-ink`}>{item.title}</h3>
        <div className={`${dense ? "mt-2 text-xs" : "mt-3 text-sm"} flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-5 gap-y-2 text-muted`}>
          <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5"><Icon name="user" size="size-4" /> {item.author}</span>
          <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 font-semibold"><Icon name="file" size="size-4" /> {item.size}</span>
        </div>
      </div>
      <Button onClick={() => onDownload(item)} variant="dark" className={`${list ? "w-full sm:w-auto" : dense ? "mt-4 w-full px-2 py-2 text-xs" : "mt-6 w-full"}`}>
        <Icon name="download" size="size-4" /> {dense ? "Download" : "Download PDF"}
      </Button>
    </article>
  );
}

import { Icon } from "./Icon";

export function SelectField({
  value,
  onChange,
  label,
  options,
}: {
  value: string;
  onChange: (value: string) => void;
  label: string;
  options: string[];
}) {
  return (
    // Ganti 'flex-1' dengan 'w-full md:w-52 shrink-0'
    <label className="relative block w-full shrink-0 md:w-52">
      <span className="sr-only">{label}</span>
      <select
        value={value}
        onChange={(event) => onChange(event.target.value)}
        className="h-12 w-full appearance-none truncate rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white pl-4 pr-9 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700 outline-none transition focus:border-accent focus:ring-4 focus:ring-accent/15"
      >
        <option value="">{label}</option>
        {options.map((option) => (
          <option key={option}>{option}</option>
        ))}
      </select>
      <span className="pointer-events-none absolute right-3 top-3.5 text-slate-400">
        <Icon name="arrowDown" size="size-4" />
      </span>
    </label>
  );
}

FILE: src/lib/authContext.tsx
================================================
import { useEffect, useState, createContext, useContext, ReactNode } from "react";
import { User, Session } from "@supabase/supabase-js";
import { supabase } from "./supabase";

export interface UserProfile {
  id: string;
  nim?: string;
  full_name: string;
  role: "student" | "librarian";
  prodi?: string;
  avatar_url?: string;
  clear_status: boolean;
}

interface AuthContextType {
  user: User | null;
  profile: UserProfile | null;
  session: Session | null;
  loading: boolean;
  signOut: () => Promise<void>;
  refreshProfile: () => Promise<void>;
}

const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextType>({
  user: null,
  profile: null,
  session: null,
  loading: true,
  signOut: async () => {},
  refreshProfile: async () => {},
});

export function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [session, setSession] = useState<Session | null>(null);
  const [user, setUser] = useState<User | null>(null);
  const [profile, setProfile] = useState<UserProfile | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  const fetchProfile = async (userId: string) => {
    try {
      const { data, error } = await supabase
        .from("profiles")
        .select("*")
        .eq("id", userId)
        .single();

      if (!error && data) {
        setProfile(data as UserProfile);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Error fetching user profile:", err);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    supabase.auth.getSession().then(({ data: { session } }) => {
      setSession(session);
      setUser(session?.user ?? null);
      if (session?.user) {
        fetchProfile(session.user.id).finally(() => setLoading(false));
      } else {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    });

    const { data: { subscription } } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange((_event, session) => {
      setSession(session);
      setUser(session?.user ?? null);
      if (session?.user) {
        fetchProfile(session.user.id);
      } else {
        setProfile(null);
      }
      setLoading(false);
    });

    return () => {
      subscription.unsubscribe();
    };
  }, []);

  const signOut = async () => {
    await supabase.auth.signOut();
    setUser(null);
    setProfile(null);
    setSession(null);
  };

  const refreshProfile = async () => {
    if (user?.id) {
      await fetchProfile(user.id);
    }
  };

  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider value={{ user, profile, session, loading, signOut, refreshProfile }}>
      {children}
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useAuth() {
  return useContext(AuthContext);
}

FILE: src/lib/useSupabaseData.ts
================================================
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { supabase } from "./supabase";
import { Book, BookTopic, RepoItem } from "../types";
import { fallbackDepartments, fallbackTopics } from "./catalogTaxonomy";

export function useBooks() {
  const [items, setItems] = useState<Book[]>([]);
  const [topics, setTopics] = useState<BookTopic[]>([]);
  const [departments, setDepartments] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const fetchBooks = async () => {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      const [{ data, error: err }, { data: topicRows, error: topicError }, { data: departmentRows, error: departmentError }] = await Promise.all([
        supabase.from("books").select("*").order("created_at", { ascending: false }),
        supabase.from("book_topics").select("id,name,slug,parent_id,sort_order").order("sort_order"),
        supabase.from("departments").select("id,name,sort_order").order("sort_order"),
      ]);

      if (err) throw err;
      const taxonomyAvailable = !topicError && !departmentError;
      if (topicError || departmentError) {
        console.warn("Taxonomy tables are unavailable; using legacy book categories.", { topicError, departmentError });
      }

      const topicById = new Map<string, { name: string; parentId: string | null }>();
      const rawTopics = topicRows ?? [];
      const topicNames = new Map(rawTopics.map((row) => [String(row.id), String(row.name)]));
      for (const row of rawTopics) {
        topicById.set(String(row.id), {
          name: String(row.name),
          parentId: row.parent_id ? String(row.parent_id) : null,
        });
      }
      const departmentById = new Map<string, string>();
      for (const row of departmentRows ?? []) {
        departmentById.set(String(row.id), String(row.name));
      }

      if (data) {
        const mappedBooks: Book[] = data.map((raw) => {
          const b = raw as Record<string, unknown>;
          const legacyCategory = typeof b.category === "string" ? b.category : "Umum";
          const topic = typeof b.topic_id === "string" ? topicById.get(b.topic_id) : undefined;
          const parent = topic?.parentId ? topicById.get(topic.parentId) : undefined;
          return {
            id: String(b.id),
            code: String(b.code),
            title: String(b.title),
            author: String(b.author),
            shelf: typeof b.shelf === "string" ? b.shelf : "Umum",
            stock: typeof b.stock === "number" ? b.stock : 0,
            department: taxonomyAvailable && typeof b.department_id === "string" ? departmentById.get(b.department_id) ?? legacyCategory : legacyCategory,
            theme: taxonomyAvailable ? topic?.name ?? "Umum" : legacyCategory,
            mainTheme: taxonomyAvailable ? parent?.name ?? topic?.name ?? "Umum" : legacyCategory,
            departmentId: typeof b.department_id === "string" ? b.department_id : undefined,
            topicId: typeof b.topic_id === "string" ? b.topic_id : undefined,
            cover: typeof b.cover_url === "string" ? b.cover_url : "",
            year: typeof b.year === "number" ? b.year : new Date().getFullYear(),
          };
        });
        setItems(mappedBooks);
      }
      setDepartments(taxonomyAvailable && departmentRows?.length ? departmentRows.map((row) => row.name as string) : fallbackDepartments);
      setTopics(taxonomyAvailable && rawTopics.length ? rawTopics.map((row) => ({
        id: row.id as string,
        name: row.name as string,
        slug: row.slug as string,
        parentId: (row.parent_id as string | null) ?? null,
        parentName: row.parent_id ? topicNames.get(String(row.parent_id)) ?? null : null,
        sortOrder: row.sort_order as number,
      })) : fallbackTopics);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      console.error("Error fetching books:", err);
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Gagal mengambil data buku");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchBooks();
  }, []);

  return { books: items, topics, departments, loading, error, refetch: fetchBooks, setBooks: setItems };
}

export function useRepository() {
  const [items, setItems] = useState<RepoItem[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const fetchRepoItems = async () => {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      const { data, error: err } = await supabase
        .from("repository_items")
        .select("*")
        .eq("status", "published")
        .order("created_at", { ascending: false });

      if (err) throw err;

      if (data) {
        const mapped: RepoItem[] = data.map((item) => ({
          id: item.id,
          title: item.title,
          author: item.author_name || "Mahasiswa PNL",
          department: item.prodi || "Teknologi Informasi dan Komputer",
          size: item.file_size || "Ukuran tidak tersedia",
          category: item.category || "nonfiction",
          type: item.type || "Karya Ilmiah",
          accent: item.category === "fiction" ? "bg-accent-soft text-forest" : "bg-canvas text-forest",
          year: String(item.year || 2024),
          badge: item.status === "published" ? "Terverifikasi" : "Kurasi",
          pdfUrl: item.pdf_url,
          downloadsCount: item.downloads_count ?? 0,
        }));
        setItems(mapped);
      }
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      console.error("Error fetching repository:", err);
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Gagal mengambil data repository");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchRepoItems();
  }, []);

  return { repoItems: items, loading, error, refetch: fetchRepoItems };
}

FILE: src/pages/CatalogPage.tsx
