import { useState } from "react";
import { Icon } from "../components/ui/Icon";
import { Hero } from "../components/ui/Hero";
import { SelectField } from "../components/ui/SelectField";
import { LayoutSwitcher } from "../components/ui/LayoutSwitcher";
import { BookCard } from "../components/ui/BookCard";
import { Layout, Book } from "../types";
import { useBooks } from "../lib/useSupabaseData";

export function BooksView({ onReserve, items: customItems, onManage }: { onReserve: (book: Book) => void; items?: Book[]; onManage?: (book: Book) => void }) {
  const { books: fetchedBooks, topics, departments, loading, error } = useBooks();
  const items = customItems || fetchedBooks;
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [department, setDepartment] = useState("");
  const [theme, setTheme] = useState("");
  const [subtheme, setSubtheme] = useState("");
  const [layout, setLayout] = useState<Layout>("grid4");
  const filtered = items.filter((book) => {
    const matchesQuery = `${book.title} ${book.author}`.toLowerCase().includes(query.toLowerCase());
    return matchesQuery && (!department || book.department === department) && (!theme || book.mainTheme === theme) && (!subtheme || book.theme === subtheme);
  });
  const gridClass = layout === "grid4"
  ? "grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 md:grid-cols-4"
  : layout === "grid5"
    ? "grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 md:grid-cols-4 lg:grid-cols-5"
    : layout === "grid6"
      ? "grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 md:grid-cols-4 lg:grid-cols-6"
      : "grid-cols-1";

  return (
    <div>
      <Hero eyebrow="Katalog Perpustakaan" title="Akses Koleksi Buku Fisik Perpustakaan PNL" subtitle="Cari buku, cek lokasi rak fisik, dan reservasi mandiri." icon="books" />
      <section className="relative z-10 mx-3 -mt-5 rounded-2xl border border-emerald-950/10 bg-white p-4 shadow-xl shadow-emerald-950/10 md:mx-6 md:p-5">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 md:flex-row md:items-center">
  <label className="relative min-w-0 flex-1">
    <span className="sr-only">Cari judul atau penulis</span>
    <span className="absolute left-4 top-3.5 text-slate-400">
      <Icon name="search" />
    </span>
    <input
      value={query}
      onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)}
      placeholder="Cari judul buku atau nama penulis..."
      className="h-12 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white pl-12 pr-4 text-sm font-medium text-slate-900 outline-none placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:border-accent focus:ring-4 focus:ring-accent/15"
    />
  </label>

  <SelectField
    value={department}
    onChange={setDepartment}
    label="Semua Jurusan"
    options={departments.length ? departments : [...new Set(items.map((book) => book.department))]}
  />
  <SelectField
    value={theme}
    onChange={(value) => { setTheme(value); setSubtheme(""); }}
    label="Semua Tema Buku"
    options={[...new Set(topics.filter((topic) => !topic.parentId).map((topic) => topic.name))]}
  />
  <SelectField
    value={subtheme}
    onChange={setSubtheme}
    label="Semua Subtema"
    options={[...new Set(topics.filter((topic) => topic.parentId && (!theme || topic.parentName === theme)).map((topic) => topic.name))]}
  />
  <LayoutSwitcher value={layout} onChange={setLayout} />
</div>
      </section>
      <section className="mt-9">
        <div className="mb-5 flex items-end justify-between gap-4">
          <div>
            <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-forest">Koleksi terbaru</p>
            <h2 className="mt-1 text-2xl font-black tracking-tight text-ink">
              {loading ? "Memuat katalog..." : `${filtered.length} buku ditemukan`}
            </h2>
          </div>
        </div>
        {error ? (
          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-red-300 bg-white py-16 text-center text-sm text-red-700">{error}</div>
        ) : filtered.length ? (
          <div className={`grid ${layout === "grid5" || layout === "grid6" ? "gap-3" : "gap-5"} ${gridClass}`}>{filtered.map((book) => <BookCard key={book.code} book={book} layout={layout} onReserve={onReserve} onManage={onManage} />)}</div>
        ) : (
          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-forest/20 bg-white py-16 text-center">
            <div className="mx-auto flex size-12 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-canvas text-muted"><Icon name="search" /></div>
            <h3 className="mt-4 font-bold text-ink">Buku belum ditemukan</h3>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted">Coba ubah kata kunci atau filter pencarian.</p>
          </div>
        )}
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/pages/LibrarianBookForm.tsx
================================================
import { useState, ReactNode } from "react";
import { fmtDate, rupiah } from "../data/librarianData";
import { Icon, Badge, Tabs, Btn, PageHead, Modal, Metric, SideLink, P, card, input, ROW, TIP, NavItem } from "./LibrarianShared";
import { Book } from "../types";
import { useBooks } from "../lib/useSupabaseData";


export function BookForm({ initial, onSave, onClose }: { initial?: Book; onSave: (b: Book) => void; onClose: () => void }) {
  const { topics, departments } = useBooks();
  const [f, setF] = useState<Book>(initial ?? { id: "", title: "", author: "", shelf: "", stock: 1, department: "Teknologi Informasi dan Komputer", theme: "Rekayasa Perangkat Lunak", mainTheme: "Ilmu-ilmu Terapan", cover: "", code: `BARU / ${Date.now().toString().slice(-4)}`, year: new Date().getFullYear() });
  const set = <K extends keyof Book>(k: K, v: Book[K]) => setF((p) => ({ ...p, [k]: v }));
  const field = (label: string, el: ReactNode) => <label className="block"><span className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-semibold text-muted">{label}</span>{el}</label>;
  return (
    <Modal title={initial ? "Kelola Buku" : "Tambah Buku Baru"} onClose={onClose}>
      <div className="space-y-4">
        {initial ? <div className="rounded-xl bg-canvas p-3"><p className="font-semibold">{f.title}</p><p className="text-xs text-muted">{f.author} · {f.code}</p></div> : (
          <>
            {field("Judul Buku", <input className={input} value={f.title} onChange={(e) => set("title", e.target.value)} />)}
            {field("Penulis", <input className={input} value={f.author} onChange={(e) => set("author", e.target.value)} />)}
          </>
        )}
        {field("Jumlah Stok", (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <button onClick={() => set("stock", Math.max(0, f.stock - 1))} className="size-10 rounded-xl border border-emerald-950/10 text-lg hover:bg-canvas">−</button>
            <input type="number" min={0} className={`${input} text-center tabular-nums`} value={f.stock} onChange={(e) => set("stock", Math.max(0, Number(e.target.value)))} />
            <button onClick={() => set("stock", f.stock + 1)} className="size-10 rounded-xl border border-emerald-950/10 text-lg hover:bg-canvas">+</button>
          </div>
        ))}
        {field("Lokasi Rak", <input className={`${input} font-mono`} placeholder="B-04 / TIK" value={f.shelf} onChange={(e) => set("shelf", e.target.value)} />)}
        {field("Jurusan", <select className={input} value={f.department} onChange={(e) => set("department", e.target.value)}>{(departments.length ? departments : ["Teknologi Informasi dan Komputer", "Teknik Elektro", "Teknik Mesin", "Teknik Kimia", "Teknik Sipil", "Bisnis"]).map((name) => <option key={name}>{name}</option>)}</select>)}
        {field("Tema Utama", <select className={input} value={f.mainTheme ?? ""} onChange={(e) => { set("mainTheme", e.target.value); set("theme", ""); set("topicId", undefined); }}>{topics.filter((topic) => !topic.parentId).map((topic) => <option key={topic.id}>{topic.name}</option>)}</select>)}
        {field("Subtema", <select className={input} value={f.theme} onChange={(e) => { const topic = topics.find((item) => item.name === e.target.value); set("theme", e.target.value); set("topicId", topic?.id); }}>{topics.filter((topic) => topic.parentId && topic.parentName === f.mainTheme).map((topic) => <option key={topic.id}>{topic.name}</option>)}</select>)}
      </div>
      <div className="mt-6 flex justify-end gap-3">
        <Btn kind="outline" onClick={onClose}>Batal</Btn>
        <Btn disabled={!f.title.trim() || !f.shelf.trim()} onClick={() => onSave(f)}>Simpan Perubahan</Btn>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
}

FILE: src/pages/LibrarianCatalog.tsx
================================================
import { useState, useEffect, ReactNode } from "react";
import { CatalogTab } from "../data/librarianData";
import { Icon, Badge, Tabs, Btn, PageHead, Modal, Metric, SideLink, P, card, input, ROW, TIP, NavItem } from "./LibrarianShared";
import { Book } from "../types";
import { BooksView } from "./CatalogPage";
import { RepositoryView } from "./RepositoryPage";
import { BookForm } from "./LibrarianBookForm";
import { supabase } from "../lib/supabase";

export function Catalog({ items, setItems, toast, refetch }: { items: Book[]; setItems: (fn: (b: Book[]) => Book[]) => void; toast: (m: string) => void; refetch: () => Promise<void> }) {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<CatalogTab>("books");
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<Book | null>(null);
  const [adding, setAdding] = useState(false);
  return (
    <>
      <div className="mb-6 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <Tabs value={tab} onChange={setTab} items={[["books", "Buku Fisik"], ["erepo", "E-Repository"]]} />
        {tab === "books" && <Btn kind="lime" onClick={() => setAdding(true)}><Icon d="plus" className="size-4" />Tambah Buku Baru</Btn>}
      </div>
      {tab === "books" ? <BooksView items={items} onReserve={() => {}} onManage={setEditing} /> : <RepositoryView onDownload={() => toast("Salinan arsip PDF diunduh untuk pemeriksaan.")} />}
      {editing && <BookForm initial={editing} onClose={() => setEditing(null)} onSave={async (b) => {
        const { error } = await supabase.from("books").update({ title: b.title, author: b.author, category: b.department, department_id: b.departmentId ?? null, topic_id: b.topicId ?? null, year: b.year, stock: b.stock, shelf: b.shelf, cover_url: b.cover || null }).eq("id", b.id);
        if (error) return toast(error.message);
        await refetch();
        setEditing(null);
        toast(`Stok "${b.title}" diperbarui: ${b.stock} eksemplar · ${b.shelf}`);
      }} />}
      {adding && <BookForm onClose={() => setAdding(false)} onSave={async (b) => {
        const { data, error } = await supabase.from("books").insert({ code: b.code, title: b.title, author: b.author, category: b.department, department_id: b.departmentId ?? null, topic_id: b.topicId ?? null, year: b.year, stock: b.stock, shelf: b.shelf, cover_url: b.cover || null }).select("*").single();
        if (error) return toast(error.message);
        await refetch();
        setAdding(false);
        toast(`"${b.title}" ditambahkan ke katalog.`);
      }} />}
    </>
  );
}

import { useState } from "react";
import { Submission } from "../data/librarianData";
import { Icon, Badge, Tabs, Btn, PageHead, Modal, card, input } from "./LibrarianShared";

export function Curation({ subs, loading, error, onPublish, onRevise }: { subs: Submission[]; loading: boolean; error: string | null; onPublish: (submission: Submission) => void; onRevise: (submission: Submission, note: string) => void }) {
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<"all" | "Non-Fiksi" | "Fiksi">("all");
  const [revise, setRevise] = useState<Submission | null>(null);
  const [note, setNote] = useState("");
  const list = subs.filter((item) => filter === "all" || item.category === filter);
  return <>
    <PageHead eyebrow="Quality Control" title="Kurasi E-Repository" sub="Saring karya sebelum dipublikasikan." right={<Tabs value={filter} onChange={setFilter} items={[["all", `Semua · ${subs.length}`], ["Non-Fiksi", "Non-Fiksi"], ["Fiksi", "Fiksi"]]} />} />
    {error && <p role="alert" className="mb-4 rounded-xl bg-danger-soft p-4 text-sm text-danger">{error}</p>}
    {loading && <p className={`${card} p-10 text-center text-sm text-muted`}>Memuat antrean kurasi...</p>}
    {!loading && list.length === 0 && <p className={`${card} p-10 text-center text-sm text-muted`}>Antrean kurasi kosong.</p>}
    <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2 2xl:grid-cols-3">{!loading && list.map((item) => <article key={item.id} className={`${card} flex flex-col p-5`}><div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2"><Badge tone={item.category === "Fiksi" ? "lime" : "slate"}>{item.category}</Badge><span className="text-xs text-muted">{item.type} · {item.size}</span></div><h3 className="mt-3 font-bold leading-snug">{item.title}</h3><p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted">{item.author} · {item.major}</p>{item.pdfUrl && <a href={item.pdfUrl} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" className="mt-4 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm font-semibold text-forest hover:underline"><Icon d="eye" className="size-4" />Pratinjau PDF</a>}<div className="mt-auto flex gap-2 pt-5"><Btn kind="outline" className="flex-1" onClick={() => setRevise(item)}><Icon d="pen" className="size-4" />Minta Revisi</Btn><Btn className="flex-1" onClick={() => onPublish(item)}><Icon d="check" className="size-4" />Publish</Btn></div></article>)}</div>
    {revise && <Modal title="Minta Revisi" onClose={() => setRevise(null)}><p className="text-sm text-muted">Umpan balik untuk <span className="font-semibold text-ink">{revise.title}</span></p><textarea value={note} onChange={(event) => setNote(event.target.value)} rows={4} placeholder="Tulis catatan revisi..." className={`${input} mt-3 resize-none`} /><div className="mt-5 flex justify-end gap-3"><Btn kind="outline" onClick={() => setRevise(null)}>Batal</Btn><Btn disabled={!note.trim()} onClick={() => { onRevise(revise, note); setRevise(null); setNote(""); }}>Kirim Umpan Balik</Btn></div></Modal>}
  </>;
}

import { View } from "../data/librarianData";
import { Badge, Icon, Metric, PageHead, card } from "./LibrarianShared";

export function Dashboard({ go, loading, error, metrics }: { go: (view: View) => void; loading: boolean; error: string | null; metrics: { transactions: number; holds: number; fines: number; submissions: number } }) {
  const queue = [
    { label: "Verifikasi QRIS Pending", value: metrics.fines, view: "fines" as View },
    { label: "Antrean Kurasi E-Repo", value: metrics.submissions, view: "erepo_curation" as View },
  ];
  return <>
    <PageHead eyebrow={new Date().toLocaleDateString("id-ID", { weekday: "long", day: "2-digit", month: "long", year: "numeric" })} title="Dashboard Operasional" sub="Ringkasan layanan perpustakaan hari ini." />
    {error && <p role="alert" className="mb-4 rounded-xl bg-danger-soft p-4 text-sm text-danger">{error}</p>}
    {loading ? <p className={`${card} p-10 text-center text-sm text-muted`}>Memuat metrik...</p> : <><div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4"><Metric label="Transaksi Hari Ini" value={metrics.transactions} icon="repeat" note="Pinjam + kembali hari ini" onClick={() => go("sirkulasi")} /><Metric label="Antrean Hold Aktif" value={metrics.holds} icon="ticket" note="Tiket yang perlu dilayani" onClick={() => go("sirkulasi")} /><Metric label="Verifikasi QRIS Pending" value={metrics.fines} icon="receipt" note="Struk pembayaran denda" onClick={() => go("fines")} /><Metric label="Antrean Kurasi E-Repo" value={metrics.submissions} icon="fileCheck" note="Karya menunggu publikasi" onClick={() => go("erepo_curation")} /></div><section className={`${card} mt-6`}><div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-emerald-950/10 px-5 py-4"><h2 className="font-bold">Antrean Tindakan Cepat</h2><Badge tone="lime">{queue.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.value, 0)} item</Badge></div>{queue.every((item) => item.value === 0) ? <p className="p-8 text-center text-sm text-muted">Semua antrean bersih.</p> : <ul className="divide-y divide-emerald-950/10">{queue.filter((item) => item.value > 0).map((item) => <li key={item.view} className="flex items-center gap-4 px-5 py-3.5"><div className="min-w-0 flex-1"><p className="text-sm font-semibold">{item.label}</p><p className="text-xs text-muted">{item.value} item</p></div><button onClick={() => go(item.view)} className="rounded-lg px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-forest hover:bg-canvas">Proses</button></li>)}</ul>}</section></>}
  </>;
}

FILE: src/pages/LibrarianFines.tsx
================================================
import { useState } from "react";
import { FineReq, rupiah } from "../data/librarianData";
import { supabase } from "../lib/supabase";
import { Icon, Badge, Btn, PageHead, Modal, card, input } from "./LibrarianShared";

export function Fines({ fines, loading, error, onApprove, onReject }: { fines: FineReq[]; loading: boolean; error: string | null; onApprove: (fine: FineReq) => void; onReject: (fine: FineReq, note: string) => void }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState<FineReq | null>(null);
  const [rejecting, setRejecting] = useState(false);
  const [note, setNote] = useState("");
  const [receiptUrl, setReceiptUrl] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const showReceipt = async (fine: FineReq) => {
    setOpen(fine);
    setReceiptUrl(null);
    if (fine.receiptUrl) {
      const { data } = await supabase.storage.from("qris-receipts").createSignedUrl(fine.receiptUrl, 300);
      setReceiptUrl(data?.signedUrl ?? null);
    }
  };
  const close = () => { setOpen(null); setRejecting(false); setNote(""); setReceiptUrl(null); };
  return (
    <>
      <PageHead eyebrow="Keuangan Perpustakaan" title="Verifikasi Denda QRIS" sub="Cocokkan bukti pembayaran sebelum menyetel denda lunas." />
      {error && <p role="alert" className="mb-4 rounded-xl bg-danger-soft p-4 text-sm text-danger">{error}</p>}
      <div className={`${card} overflow-hidden`}>
        {loading && <p className="p-10 text-center text-sm text-muted">Memuat antrean denda...</p>}
        {!loading && fines.length === 0 && <p className="p-10 text-center text-sm text-muted">Tidak ada struk yang menunggu verifikasi.</p>}
        {!loading && fines.map((fine) => <div key={fine.id} className="grid gap-3 border-b border-emerald-950/10 px-5 py-4 last:border-0 md:grid-cols-[1.4fr_1.6fr_0.8fr_1fr_auto] md:items-center md:gap-4"><div><p className="font-semibold">{fine.name}</p><p className="font-mono text-xs text-muted">{fine.nim}</p></div><p className="text-sm text-muted">{fine.reason}</p><p className="font-bold tabular-nums">{rupiah(fine.amount)}</p><Badge tone="amber">Menunggu Verifikasi</Badge><Btn kind="outline" className="w-28" onClick={() => void showReceipt(fine)}><Icon d="eye" className="size-4" />Lihat Struk</Btn></div>)}
      </div>
      {open && <Modal title="Bukti Pembayaran QRIS" onClose={close} wide><div className="grid gap-6 md:grid-cols-[1fr_1.1fr]"><div className="rounded-xl border border-emerald-950/10 bg-canvas p-5">{receiptUrl ? <img src={receiptUrl} alt={`Bukti pembayaran ${open.name}`} className="mx-auto max-h-96 rounded-lg object-contain" /> : <p className="text-center text-sm text-muted">Bukti tidak tersedia atau sedang dimuat.</p>}</div><div className="flex flex-col"><dl className="space-y-3 text-sm">{[["Mahasiswa", `${open.name} · ${open.nim}`], ["Program Studi", open.major], ["Tagihan sistem", rupiah(open.amount)]].map(([key, value]) => <div key={key} className="flex justify-between gap-4 border-b border-emerald-950/10 pb-2"><dt className="text-muted">{key}</dt><dd className="text-right font-semibold">{value}</dd></div>)}</dl>{rejecting && <textarea value={note} onChange={(event) => setNote(event.target.value)} rows={3} placeholder="Catatan penolakan" className={`${input} mt-4 resize-none`} />}<div className="mt-auto flex flex-wrap gap-3 pt-6">{rejecting ? <><Btn kind="outline" onClick={() => setRejecting(false)}>Batal</Btn><Btn kind="danger" className="flex-1" onClick={() => { onReject(open, note); close(); }}>Kirim Penolakan</Btn></> : <><Btn kind="danger" onClick={() => setRejecting(true)}><Icon d="x" className="size-4" />Tolak</Btn><Btn className="flex-1" onClick={() => { onApprove(open); close(); }}><Icon d="check" className="size-4" />Approve</Btn></>}</div></div></div></Modal>}
    </>
  );
}

FILE: src/pages/LibrarianShared.tsx

import { ReactNode } from "react";
import { View, Hold, Loan, Txn, Student, FineReq, Submission } from "../data/librarianData";
import { Book } from "../types";

export const P = {
  grid: "M3 3h7v7H3zM14 3h7v7h-7zM3 14h7v7H3zM14 14h7v7h-7z",
  userCheck: "M9 11a4 4 0 1 0 0-8 4 4 0 0 0 0 8zM2 21v-1a6 6 0 0 1 6-6h2a6 6 0 0 1 4 1.5M16 18l2 2 4-4",
  receipt: "M5 3h14v18l-2.5-1.5L14 21l-2-1.5L10 21l-2.5-1.5L5 21zM9 8h6M9 12h6M9 16h3",
  fileCheck: "M14 3H6v18h12V7zM14 3v4h4M9 14l2 2 4-4",
  books: "M4 4h4v16H4zM10 4h4v16h-4zM16.5 4.5l3.8 1-3.9 15-3.8-1z",
  logout: "M15 4h4v16h-4M10 8l-4 4 4 4M6 12h10",
  bell: "M6 16V11a6 6 0 1 1 12 0v5l2 2H4zM10 20a2 2 0 0 0 4 0",
  search: "M11 18a7 7 0 1 0 0-14 7 7 0 0 0 0 14zM21 21l-5-5",
  menu: "M4 6h16M4 12h16M4 18h16",
  x: "M6 6l12 12M18 6L6 18",
  check: "M5 12l5 5 9-10",
  chevL: "M15 6l-6 6 6 6",
  chevR: "M9 6l6 6-6 6",
  repeat: "M17 2l4 4-4 4M3 11V9a3 3 0 0 1 3-3h15M7 22l-4-4 4-4M21 13v2a3 3 0 0 1-3 3H3",
  clock: "M12 7v5l3 2M12 21a9 9 0 1 0 0-18 9 9 0 0 0 0 18z",
  ticket: "M3 8a2 2 0 0 0 0 4v0a2 2 0 0 1 0 4v2h18v-2a2 2 0 0 1 0-4 2 2 0 0 1 0-4V6H3zM14 6v12",
  eye: "M2 12s3.5-7 10-7 10 7 10 7-3.5 7-10 7S2 12 2 12zM12 15a3 3 0 1 0 0-6 3 3 0 0 0 0 6z",
  alert: "M12 9v4M12 17h.01M10.3 3.9L2 18a2 2 0 0 0 1.7 3h16.6a2 2 0 0 0 1.7-3L13.7 3.9a2 2 0 0 0-3.4 0z",
  plus: "M12 5v14M5 12h14",
  arrowR: "M5 12h14M13 6l6 6-6 6",
  pen: "M4 20h4L19 9l-4-4L4 16zM13 7l4 4",
  shield: "M12 3l8 3v6c0 5-3.5 8-8 9-4.5-1-8-4-8-9V6z",
};
export const card = "rounded-2xl border border-emerald-950/10 bg-white";
export const input = "w-full rounded-xl border border-emerald-950/10 bg-white px-4 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none transition focus:border-forest/40 focus:ring-4 focus:ring-accent/30";
export function Icon({ d, className = "size-5" }: { d: keyof typeof P; className?: string }) {
  return (
    <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={1.5} strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" className={className} aria-hidden>
      <path d={P[d]} />
    </svg>
  );
}

export function Badge({ tone, children }: { tone: "green" | "amber" | "red" | "slate" | "lime" | "blue"; children: ReactNode }) {
  const m = {
    green: "bg-success-soft text-success",
    amber: "bg-amber-100 text-amber-800",
    red: "bg-danger-soft text-danger",
    slate: "bg-slate-100 text-muted",
    lime: "bg-accent-soft text-forest",
    blue: "bg-sky-50 text-sky-800",
  }[tone];
  return <span className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap rounded-full px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold ${m}`}>{children}</span>;
}
export function Tabs<T extends string>({ value, onChange, items }: { value: T; onChange: (v: T) => void; items: [T, string][] }) {
  return (
    <div className="inline-flex flex-wrap gap-1 rounded-xl border border-emerald-950/10 bg-white p-1">
      {items.map(([k, l]) => (
        <button key={k} onClick={() => onChange(k)} className={`rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold transition ${value === k ? "bg-forest text-white" : "text-muted hover:bg-canvas hover:text-ink"}`}>{l}</button>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

export function Btn({ kind = "primary", className = "", children, ...rest }: { kind?: "primary" | "outline" | "lime" | "danger" } & React.ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement>) {
  const k = {
    primary: "bg-forest text-white hover:bg-forest-light",
    lime: "bg-accent text-forest hover:bg-accent-hover",
    outline: "border border-emerald-950/15 bg-white text-ink hover:border-forest/30 hover:bg-canvas",
    danger: "border border-danger/25 bg-white text-danger hover:bg-danger-soft",
  }[kind];
  return <button {...rest} className={`inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold transition focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-forest disabled:opacity-45 ${k} ${className}`}>{children}</button>;
}
export function PageHead({ eyebrow, title, sub, right }: { eyebrow: string; title: string; sub: string; right?: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <header className="flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-4 pb-6">
      <div>
        <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.15em] text-forest-light">{eyebrow}</p>
        <h1 className="mt-1 text-2xl font-extrabold tracking-tight md:text-3xl">{title}</h1>
        <p className="mt-1 max-w-2xl text-sm text-muted">{sub}</p>
      </div>
      {right}
    </header>
  );
}

export function Modal({ title, onClose, children, wide }: { title: string; onClose: () => void; children: ReactNode; wide?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 grid place-items-center bg-forest/50 p-4 backdrop-blur-sm" onClick={onClose}>
      <div role="dialog" aria-modal className={`${card} max-h-[92vh] w-full overflow-y-auto p-6 shadow-2xl ${wide ? "max-w-3xl" : "max-w-md"}`} onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
        <div className="mb-5 flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-extrabold tracking-tight">{title}</h2>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="rounded-lg p-1.5 text-muted hover:bg-canvas" aria-label="Tutup"><Icon d="x" /></button>
        </div>
        {children}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export function Metric({ label, value, icon, note, onClick }: { label: string; value: number; icon: keyof typeof P; note: string; onClick: () => void }) {
  return (
    <button onClick={onClick} className={`${card} group p-5 text-left transition hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:border-forest/25`}>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <span className="grid size-10 place-items-center rounded-xl bg-accent-soft text-forest"><Icon d={icon} /></span>
        <Icon d="arrowR" className="size-4 text-muted opacity-0 transition group-hover:opacity-100" />
      </div>
      <p className="mt-5 text-3xl font-extrabold tabular-nums tracking-tight">{value}</p>
      <p className="mt-1 text-sm font-semibold">{label}</p>
      <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-muted">{note}</p>
    </button>
  );
}

export function SideLink({ item, active, collapsed, onClick }: { item: NavItem; active: boolean; collapsed: boolean; onClick: () => void }) {
  return (
    <button onClick={onClick} aria-label={item.label} aria-current={active ? "page" : undefined}
      className={`${ROW} ${collapsed ? "lg:justify-center lg:px-0" : ""} ${active ? "bg-accent font-semibold text-forest" : "text-white/70 hover:bg-white/10 hover:text-white"}`}>
      <Icon d={item.icon} className="size-5 shrink-0" />
      <span className={`flex-1 truncate text-left ${collapsed ? "lg:hidden" : ""}`}>{item.label}</span>
      {!!item.count && <span className={`rounded-full px-2 text-[11px] font-bold tabular-nums ${active ? "bg-forest/10" : "bg-white/10"} ${collapsed ? "lg:absolute lg:right-1 lg:top-1 lg:px-1.5" : ""}`}>{item.count}</span>}
      {collapsed && <span role="tooltip" className={TIP}>{item.label}</span>}
    </button>
  );
}


export type NavItem = { key: View; label: string; icon: keyof typeof P; count?: number };
export const TIP = "pointer-events-none invisible absolute left-full z-50 ml-3 hidden whitespace-nowrap rounded-md bg-slate-900 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs text-white opacity-0 shadow-lg transition-opacity duration-150 group-hover:visible group-hover:opacity-100 group-focus-visible:visible group-focus-visible:opacity-100 lg:block";
export const ROW = "group relative flex min-h-[42px] w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-xl px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium tracking-[-0.01em] transition-[background-color,color] duration-150 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-accent";

import { useState } from "react";
import { CircTab, Student, rupiah } from "../data/librarianData";

export function Sirkulasi({
  student,
  query,
  setQuery,
  loading,
  error,

  onHandover,
  onReturn,
}: {
  student: Student | null;
  query: string;
  setQuery: (q: string) => void;
  loading: boolean;
  error: string | null;
  onSearch: (q: string) => void;
  onHandover: (holdId: string) => void;
  onReturn: (loanId: string) => void;
}) {

    <>

        <div className="relative flex-1">
          <Icon d="search" className="absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 size-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted" />

      </form>

            </div>
            {[["Pinjaman Aktif", `${student.loans.filter((loan) => !loan.returned_at).length} buku`], ["Tiket Hold", `${student.holds.length} aktif`]].map(([label, value]) => <div key={label} className="border-emerald-950/10 lg:border-l lg:pl-5"><p className="text-xs text-muted">{label}</p><p className="mt-1 text-lg font-extrabold">{value}</p></div>)}
            <div className="border-emerald-950/10 lg:border-l lg:pl-5"><p className="text-xs text-muted">Denda & Bebas Pustaka</p><div className="mt-1.5">{student.clear ? <Badge tone="green"><Icon d="check" className="size-3.5" />Bebas Pustaka</Badge> : <Badge tone="red"><Icon d="alert" className="size-3.5" />{rupiah(student.fine)} tertunggak</Badge>}</div></div>
          </section>
          <div className="mt-6"><Tabs value={tab} onChange={setTab} items={[["hold", `Antrean Hold · ${student.holds.length}`], ["loans", `Buku Dipinjam · ${student.loans.filter((loan) => !loan.returned_at).length}`], ["history", "Riwayat Transaksi"]]} /></div>
          <div className="mt-4 space-y-3">
            {tab === "hold" && (student.holds.length ? student.holds.map((hold) => <div key={hold.id} className={`${card} flex flex-col gap-4 p-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center`}><span className="grid size-10 place-items-center rounded-xl bg-accent-soft text-forest"><Icon d="ticket" /></span><div className="min-w-0 flex-1"><p className="font-semibold">{hold.title}</p><p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-muted"><span className="font-mono">{hold.pin}</span> · {hold.shelf} · {hold.code}</p></div><Badge tone="amber"><Icon d="clock" className="size-3.5" />s.d. {hold.expires}</Badge><Btn kind="lime" onClick={() => onHandover(hold.id)}><Icon d="check" className="size-4" />Serahkan Buku</Btn></div>) : <p className={`${card} p-6 text-center text-sm text-muted`}>Tidak ada tiket hold aktif.</p>)}
            {tab === "loans" && (student.loans.filter((loan) => !loan.returned_at).length ? student.loans.filter((loan) => !loan.returned_at).map((loan) => <div key={loan.id} className={`${card} flex flex-col gap-3 p-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center`}><div className="min-w-0 flex-1"><p className="font-semibold">{loan.title}</p><p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-muted">Dipinjam {loan.start} · jatuh tempo {loan.due}</p></div><Badge tone={loan.daysLeft <= 3 ? "amber" : "green"}>{loan.daysLeft} hari lagi</Badge><Btn kind="outline" onClick={() => onReturn(loan.id)}>Terima Pengembalian</Btn></div>) : <p className={`${card} p-6 text-center text-sm text-muted`}>Tidak ada pinjaman aktif.</p>)}
            {tab === "history" && <div className={card}>{student.history.length ? student.history.map((transaction, index) => <div key={`${transaction.date}-${index}`} className="flex items-center gap-4 border-b border-emerald-950/10 px-5 py-3 text-sm last:border-0"><span className="w-24 shrink-0 text-xs text-muted">{transaction.date}</span><span className="flex-1 font-semibold">{transaction.title}</span><span className="text-xs text-muted">{transaction.action}</span></div>) : <p className="p-6 text-center text-sm text-muted">Belum ada riwayat transaksi.</p>}</div>}
          </div>
        </>
      )}
    </>
  );
}

FILE: src/pages/QrisMock.tsx
================================================
import { useState, useEffect, ReactNode } from "react";
import { Icon, Badge, Cover, Title, Stat, useCountdown, btnPrimary, btnSecondary, btnGhost, card, FINE_RATE, rupiah, Modal, UploadModal, QrisModal } from "./StudentShared";
import { StudentBook, Paper, papers } from "../data/portalData";
import { Icon as GlobalIcon } from "../components/ui/Icon";
import { Button } from "../components/ui/Button";

export function QrisMock() {
  const n = 25;
  const cells = Array.from({ length: n * n }, (_, i) => ((i * 7919) ^ ((i >> 2) * 131)) % 3 === 0);
  const finder = (r: number, c: number) => {
    const inF = (rr: number, cc: number) => rr >= 0 && rr < 7 && cc >= 0 && cc < 7;
    for (const [or, oc] of [[0, 0], [0, n - 7], [n - 7, 0]]) {
      const lr = r - or, lc = c - oc;
      if (inF(lr, lc)) return lr === 0 || lr === 6 || lc === 0 || lc === 6 || (lr >= 2 && lr <= 4 && lc >= 2 && lc <= 4) ? 1 : 0;
    }
    return -1;
  };
  return (
    <div className="relative grid size-48 grid-cols-[repeat(25,1fr)] p-1">
      {cells.map((on, i) => {
        const f = finder(Math.floor(i / n), i % n);
        return <div key={i} className={(f === -1 ? on : f === 1) ? "bg-slate-900" : ""} />;
      })}
      <div className="absolute left-1/2 top-1/2 grid size-10 -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 place-items-center rounded-md border-2 border-white bg-[#0d372b] text-[9px] font-bold tracking-tight text-white">PNL</div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useState } from "react";
import { Icon } from "../components/ui/Icon";
import { Hero } from "../components/ui/Hero";
import { SelectField } from "../components/ui/SelectField";
import { LayoutSwitcher } from "../components/ui/LayoutSwitcher";
import { RepoCard } from "../components/ui/RepoCard";
import { Layout } from "../types";
import { useRepository } from "../lib/useSupabaseData";
import { RepoItem } from "../types";
import { fallbackDepartments } from "../lib/catalogTaxonomy";

export function RepositoryView({ onDownload }: { onDownload: (item: RepoItem) => void }) {
  const { repoItems, loading } = useRepository();
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [department, setDepartment] = useState("");
  const [category, setCategory] = useState<"all" | "nonfiction" | "fiction">("all");
  const [layout, setLayout] = useState<Layout>("grid4");

  // Filter logika pencarian, jurusan, dan kategori
  const filtered = repoItems.filter((item) => {
    const matchesQuery = `${item.title} ${item.author}`.toLowerCase().includes(query.toLowerCase());
    const matchesDepartment = !department || item.department === department;
    const matchesCategory = category === "all" || item.category === category;
    return matchesQuery && matchesDepartment && matchesCategory;
  });

  // Breakpoint Grid yang lebih responsif di zoom 100%
  const gridClass =
    layout === "grid4"
      ? "grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-4"
      : layout === "grid5"
        ? "grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 md:grid-cols-4 lg:grid-cols-5"
        : layout === "grid6"
          ? "grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 md:grid-cols-4 lg:grid-cols-6"
          : "grid-cols-1";

  return (
    <div>
      <Hero
        eyebrow="E-Repository PNL"
        title="Repositori Karya Ilmiah & Kreatif Mahasiswa"
        subtitle="Unggah dan akses karya digital mahasiswa Politeknik Negeri Lhokseumawe."
        icon="layers"
      />

      {/* SEARCH BAR & FILTER CONTROL SECTION */}
      <section className="relative z-10 mx-3 -mt-5 rounded-2xl border border-emerald-950/10 bg-white p-4 shadow-xl shadow-emerald-950/10 md:mx-6 md:p-5">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 md:flex-row md:items-center">
          {/* Search Bar Utama (Memanjang / Flex-1) */}
          <label className="relative min-w-0 flex-1">
            <span className="sr-only">Cari karya digital</span>
            <span className="absolute left-4 top-3.5 text-slate-400">
              <Icon name="search" />
            </span>
            <input
              value={query}
              onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)}
              placeholder="Cari judul paper, laporan magang, cerpen, atau penulis..."
              className="h-12 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white pl-12 pr-4 text-sm font-medium text-slate-900 outline-none placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:border-emerald-700 focus:ring-4 focus:ring-emerald-700/15"
            />
          </label>

          {/* Filter Jurusan (Width Pas/Fixed) */}
          <SelectField
            value={department}
            onChange={setDepartment}
            label="Semua Jurusan"
            options={[
              ...new Set([
                ...fallbackDepartments,
                ...repoItems
                  .map((item) => item.department)
                  .filter((department) => department.trim().length > 0),
              ]),
            ]}
          />

          {/* Toggle Segmented Button Kategori (Semua / Non-Fiksi / Fiksi) */}
          <div className="flex shrink-0 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 p-1">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setCategory("all")}
              className={`rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold transition ${
                category === "all" ? "bg-white text-slate-950 shadow-sm" : "text-slate-500"
              }`}
            >
              Semua
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setCategory("nonfiction")}
              className={`rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold transition ${
                category === "nonfiction" ? "bg-white text-slate-950 shadow-sm" : "text-slate-500"
              }`}
            >
              Non-Fiksi
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setCategory("fiction")}
              className={`rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold transition ${
                category === "fiction" ? "bg-white text-slate-950 shadow-sm" : "text-slate-500"
              }`}
            >
              Fiksi
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Layout Switcher Grid/List */}
          <LayoutSwitcher value={layout} onChange={setLayout} />
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* RENDER HASIL E-REPOSITORY */}
      <section className="mt-9">
        <div className="mb-5 flex items-end justify-between gap-4">
          <div>
            <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-forest">Arsip digital mahasiswa</p>
            <h2 className="mt-1 text-2xl font-black tracking-tight text-ink">
              {loading ? "Memuat repositori..." : `${filtered.length} berkas ditemukan`}
            </h2>
          </div>
        </div>

        {filtered.length ? (
          <div className={`grid ${layout === "grid5" || layout === "grid6" ? "gap-3" : "gap-5"} ${gridClass}`}>
            {filtered.map((item) => (
              <RepoCard key={item.title} item={item} layout={layout} onDownload={onDownload} />
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-slate-300 bg-white py-16 text-center">
            <div className="mx-auto flex size-12 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-slate-100 text-slate-500">
              <Icon name="search" />
            </div>
            <h3 className="mt-4 font-bold text-slate-950">Berkas tidak ditemukan</h3>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">Coba ubah kata kunci atau filter kategori Anda.</p>
          </div>
        )}
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/pages/StudentCirculation.tsx
================================================
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useAuth } from "../lib/authContext";
import { supabase } from "../lib/supabase";

type Row = { id: string; borrowed_at?: string; due_date?: string; returned_at?: string | null; expires_at?: string; pin?: string; status: string; books?: { title?: string; author?: string; shelf?: string } };

export function Circulation() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<"active" | "hold" | "history">("active");
  const [loans, setLoans] = useState<Row[]>([]);
  const [holds, setHolds] = useState<Row[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const load = async () => {
    if (!user) return;
    setLoading(true);
    const [loanResult, holdResult] = await Promise.all([
      supabase.from("loans").select("id,borrowed_at,due_date,returned_at,status,books(title,author,shelf)").eq("user_id", user.id).order("borrowed_at", { ascending: false }),
      supabase.from("holds").select("id,expires_at,pin,status,books(title,author,shelf)").eq("user_id", user.id).order("created_at", { ascending: false }),
    ]);
    const firstError = loanResult.error || holdResult.error;
    setError(firstError?.message ?? null);
    setLoans((loanResult.data ?? []) as Row[]);
    setHolds((holdResult.data ?? []) as Row[]);
    setLoading(false);
  };
  useEffect(() => { void load(); }, [user]);

  if (loading) return <div className="rounded-2xl border border-emerald-950/10 bg-white p-10 text-center text-sm text-muted">Memuat sirkulasi...</div>;
  if (error) return <div role="alert" className="rounded-2xl border border-danger/20 bg-danger-soft p-6 text-sm text-danger">{error}</div>;
  const active = loans.filter((loan) => !loan.returned_at);
  const history = loans.filter((loan) => loan.returned_at);
  const list = tab === "active" ? active : tab === "hold" ? holds : history;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <header><p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-forest">Sirkulasi Buku</p><h1 className="mt-1 text-2xl font-black">Peminjaman & reservasi</h1></header>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 rounded-xl bg-white p-1 shadow-sm">
        {([["active", `Sedang Dipinjam (${active.length})`], ["hold", `Hold (${holds.length})`], ["history", `Riwayat (${history.length})`]] as const).map(([key, label]) => <button key={key} onClick={() => setTab(key)} className={`rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold ${tab === key ? "bg-forest text-white" : "text-muted hover:bg-canvas"}`}>{label}</button>)}
      </div>
      {list.length ? list.map((item) => (
        <article key={item.id} className="rounded-2xl border border-emerald-950/10 bg-white p-5">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-4"><div><h2 className="font-bold">{item.books?.title ?? "Buku"}</h2><p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted">{item.books?.author ?? "Penulis tidak tersedia"} · {item.books?.shelf ?? "Rak tidak tersedia"}</p></div><span className="rounded-full bg-accent-soft px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-forest">{tab === "hold" ? "Hold aktif" : item.returned_at ? "Selesai" : "Dipinjam"}</span></div>
          <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-x-6 gap-y-2 text-sm text-muted">
            {item.borrowed_at && <span>Dipinjam {new Date(item.borrowed_at).toLocaleDateString("id-ID")}</span>}
            {item.due_date && <span>Jatuh tempo {new Date(item.due_date).toLocaleDateString("id-ID")}</span>}
            {item.returned_at && <span>Dikembalikan {new Date(item.returned_at).toLocaleDateString("id-ID")}</span>}
            {item.expires_at && <span>Kedaluwarsa {new Date(item.expires_at).toLocaleDateString("id-ID")}</span>}
            {item.pin && <span className="font-mono">{item.pin}</span>}
          </div>
        </article>
      )) : <div className="rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-forest/20 bg-white p-12 text-center text-sm text-muted">Belum ada data pada tab ini.</div>}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/pages/StudentDashboard.tsx
================================================
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useAuth } from "../lib/authContext";
import { supabase } from "../lib/supabase";
import { View } from "../data/portalData";

type DashboardRow = {
  id: string;
  due_date?: string;
  expires_at?: string;
  returned_at?: string | null;
  status?: string;
  books?: { title?: string; category?: string; author?: string; shelf?: string };
};

export function StudentDashboard({ go }: { go: (v: View) => void }) {
  const { user, profile } = useAuth();
  const [loans, setLoans] = useState<DashboardRow[]>([]);
  const [holds, setHolds] = useState<DashboardRow[]>([]);
  const [fines, setFines] = useState<{ amount: number; status: string }[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user) return;
    Promise.all([
      supabase.from("loans").select("id,due_date,returned_at,status,books(title,category,author,shelf)").eq("user_id", user.id),
      supabase.from("holds").select("id,expires_at,status,books(title,category,author,shelf)").eq("user_id", user.id).eq("status", "active"),
      supabase.from("fines").select("amount,status").eq("user_id", user.id),
    ]).then(([loanResult, holdResult, fineResult]) => {
      const firstError = loanResult.error || holdResult.error || fineResult.error;
      if (firstError) setError(firstError.message);
      setLoans((loanResult.data ?? []) as DashboardRow[]);
      setHolds((holdResult.data ?? []) as DashboardRow[]);
      setFines((fineResult.data ?? []) as { amount: number; status: string }[]);
      setLoading(false);
    });
  }, [user]);

  const activeLoans = loans.filter((loan) => !loan.returned_at);
  const outstanding = fines.filter((fine) => fine.status !== "paid").reduce((sum, fine) => sum + Number(fine.amount), 0);
  const nextLoan = useMemo(() => activeLoans.filter((loan) => loan.due_date).sort((a, b) => Date.parse(a.due_date!) - Date.parse(b.due_date!))[0], [activeLoans]);
  const genres = useMemo(() => {
    const counts = new Map<string, number>();
    loans.forEach((loan) => {
      const category = loan.books?.category || "Lainnya";
      counts.set(category, (counts.get(category) ?? 0) + 1);
    });
    return [...counts.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]);
  }, [loans]);

  if (loading) return <div className="rounded-2xl border border-emerald-950/10 bg-white p-10 text-center text-sm text-muted">Memuat dashboard...</div>;
  if (error) return <div role="alert" className="rounded-2xl border border-danger/20 bg-danger-soft p-6 text-sm text-danger">{error}</div>;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <header>
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-light tracking-tight">Selamat datang, <span className="font-semibold text-forest">{profile?.full_name}.</span></h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted">{profile?.nim ?? "Mahasiswa"} · {profile?.prodi ?? "PNL"}</p>
      </header>
      <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
        {[
          ["Pinjaman Aktif", activeLoans.length, "buku"],
          ["Hold Aktif", holds.length, "tiket"],
          ["Denda Berjalan", `Rp ${outstanding.toLocaleString("id-ID")}`, ""],
          ["Bebas Pustaka", profile?.clear_status ? "Ya" : "Tidak", ""],
        ].map(([label, value, unit]) => (
          <div key={label} className="rounded-2xl border border-emerald-950/10 bg-white p-5">
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-muted">{label}</p>
            <p className="mt-3 text-2xl font-bold text-forest">{value} <span className="text-sm font-medium text-muted">{unit}</span></p>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div className="grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-2">
        <section className="rounded-2xl border border-emerald-950/10 bg-white p-6">
          <h2 className="font-bold">Jatuh tempo terdekat</h2>
          {nextLoan ? (
            <div className="mt-4"><p className="font-semibold">{nextLoan.books?.title}</p><p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted">Jatuh tempo {new Date(nextLoan.due_date!).toLocaleDateString("id-ID")}</p></div>
          ) : <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-muted">Tidak ada pinjaman aktif.</p>}
        </section>
        <section className="rounded-2xl border border-emerald-950/10 bg-white p-6">
          <h2 className="font-bold">Hold aktif</h2>
          {holds.length ? holds.map((hold) => <div key={hold.id} className="mt-4 flex justify-between gap-4 text-sm"><span className="font-semibold">{hold.books?.title}</span><span className="text-muted">{hold.expires_at ? `s.d. ${new Date(hold.expires_at).toLocaleDateString("id-ID")}` : "Aktif"}</span></div>) : <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-muted">Belum ada reservasi aktif.</p>}
        </section>
      </div>
      <section className="rounded-2xl border border-emerald-950/10 bg-white p-6">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between"><h2 className="font-bold">Analitik koleksi</h2><button onClick={() => go("circulation")} className="text-sm font-semibold text-forest hover:underline">Lihat sirkulasi</button></div>
        {genres.length ? <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-3">{genres.map(([genre, count]) => <span key={genre} className="rounded-full bg-accent-soft px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-forest">{genre}: {count}</span>)}</div> : <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-muted">Belum ada riwayat peminjaman untuk dianalisis.</p>}
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/pages/StudentERepo.tsx
================================================
import { FormEvent, useEffect, useRef, useState, type ChangeEvent, type DragEvent } from "react";
import { useAuth } from "../lib/authContext";
import { supabase } from "../lib/supabase";

type Item = { id: string; title: string; type: string; category: string; status: string; created_at: string };
const MAX_FILE_SIZE = 10 * 1024 * 1024;

const formatSize = (size: number) => {
  if (size < 1024 * 1024) return `${(size / 1024).toFixed(0)} KB`;
  return `${(size / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(2)} MB`;
};

export function ERepo() {
  const { user, profile } = useAuth();
  const [items, setItems] = useState<Item[]>([]);
  const [title, setTitle] = useState("");
  const [type, setType] = useState("Laporan Magang");
  const [category, setCategory] = useState<"nonfiction" | "fiction">("nonfiction");
  const [file, setFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [fileError, setFileError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [dragging, setDragging] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  const load = async () => {
    if (!user) return;
    setLoading(true);
    const { data, error } = await supabase.from("repository_items").select("id,title,type,category,status,created_at").eq("author_id", user.id).order("created_at", { ascending: false });
    setItems((data ?? []) as Item[]);
    if (error) setMessage(error.message);
    setLoading(false);
  };
  useEffect(() => { void load(); }, [user]);

  const selectFile = (candidate: File | null) => {
    setFileError(null);
    if (!candidate) return;
    const isPdf = candidate.type === "application/pdf" || candidate.name.toLowerCase().endsWith(".pdf");
    if (!isPdf) {
      setFile(null);
      setFileError("File harus berformat PDF.");
      return;
    }
    if (candidate.size > MAX_FILE_SIZE) {
      setFile(null);
      setFileError("Ukuran file maksimal 10 MB.");
      return;
    }
    setFile(candidate);
  };

  const onFileChange = (event: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    selectFile(event.target.files?.[0] ?? null);
  };

  const onDrop = (event: DragEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setDragging(false);
    selectFile(event.dataTransfer.files[0] ?? null);
  };

  const submit = async (event: FormEvent) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (!user || !file || !title.trim()) return setMessage("Judul dan file PDF wajib diisi.");
    setMessage(null);
    setSubmitting(true);
    const path = `${user.id}/${Date.now()}-${file.name}`;
    const uploadResult = await supabase.storage.from("repository-files").upload(path, file, { upsert: false, contentType: "application/pdf" });
    if (uploadResult.error) {
      setSubmitting(false);
      return setMessage(uploadResult.error.message);
    }
    const { error } = await supabase.from("repository_items").insert({
      author_id: user.id, title: title.trim(), author_name: profile?.full_name, prodi: profile?.prodi ?? "PNL",
      year: new Date().getFullYear(), abstract: "", category, type, status: "pending_curation", pdf_url: path, file_name: file.name, file_size: `${(file.size / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(2)} MB`,
    });
    if (error) {
      await supabase.storage.from("repository-files").remove([path]);
      setSubmitting(false);
      return setMessage(error.message);
    }
    setTitle(""); setFile(null); setFileError(null); setMessage("Karya dikirim untuk kurasi."); setSubmitting(false);
    if (fileInputRef.current) fileInputRef.current.value = "";
    await load();
  };

  return <div className="space-y-5"><header><p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-forest">E-Repository & Karya</p><h1 className="mt-1 text-2xl font-black">Arsipkan karya ilmiahmu</h1></header><form onSubmit={(event) => void submit(event)} className="grid gap-4 rounded-2xl border border-emerald-950/10 bg-white p-6"><label className="text-sm font-semibold">Judul karya<input required value={title} onChange={(event) => setTitle(event.target.value)} className="mt-2 h-11 w-full rounded-lg border border-emerald-950/15 px-3 outline-none focus:border-accent" /></label><div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2"><label className="text-sm font-semibold">Jenis<select value={type} onChange={(event) => setType(event.target.value)} className="mt-2 h-11 w-full rounded-lg border border-emerald-950/15 px-3"><option>Laporan Magang</option><option>Skripsi</option><option>Makalah</option><option>Cerpen</option><option>Karya Kreatif</option></select></label><label className="text-sm font-semibold">Kategori<select value={category} onChange={(event) => setCategory(event.target.value as "nonfiction" | "fiction")} className="mt-2 h-11 w-full rounded-lg border border-emerald-950/15 px-3"><option value="nonfiction">Non-fiksi</option><option value="fiction">Fiksi</option></select></label></div><div><p className="text-sm font-semibold">PDF</p><div role="button" tabIndex={0} onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()} onKeyDown={(event) => { if (event.key === "Enter" || event.key === " ") fileInputRef.current?.click(); }} onDragEnter={(event) => { event.preventDefault(); setDragging(true); }} onDragOver={(event) => event.preventDefault()} onDragLeave={() => setDragging(false)} onDrop={onDrop} className={`mt-2 rounded-2xl border-2 border-dashed p-6 text-center transition ${dragging ? "border-accent bg-accent-soft" : "border-forest/20 bg-canvas"} focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-accent`}><input ref={fileInputRef} required={!file} type="file" accept="application/pdf,.pdf" onChange={onFileChange} className="sr-only" aria-label="Pilih file PDF" />{file ? <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-3 text-left"><span className="grid size-12 place-items-center rounded-xl bg-danger-soft text-danger">PDF</span><div className="min-w-0"><p className="truncate font-bold">{file.name}</p><p className="text-sm text-muted">{formatSize(file.size)}</p></div><button type="button" onClick={(event) => { event.stopPropagation(); setFile(null); setFileError(null); if (fileInputRef.current) fileInputRef.current.value = ""; }} className="rounded-lg border border-forest/15 px-3 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-forest hover:bg-white">Hapus</button><button type="button" onClick={(event) => { event.stopPropagation(); fileInputRef.current?.click(); }} className="rounded-lg bg-forest px-3 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white">Ganti file</button></div> : <div className="space-y-2"><div className="mx-auto grid size-12 place-items-center rounded-xl bg-forest text-lg font-black text-accent">↑</div><p className="font-bold">Pilih atau unggah file PDF</p><p className="text-sm text-muted">Seret file PDF ke sini atau klik untuk memilih dari perangkat.</p><span className="inline-flex rounded-lg bg-forest px-4 py-2 text-sm font-bold text-white">Pilih File PDF</span><p className="text-xs text-muted">PDF, maksimal 10 MB</p></div>}</div>{fileError && <p role="alert" className="mt-2 text-sm font-semibold text-danger">{fileError}</p>}</div><button disabled={submitting} className="rounded-lg bg-forest px-4 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60" type="submit">{submitting ? "Mengirim..." : "Kirim untuk kurasi"}</button>{message && <p role="status" className="text-sm text-muted">{message}</p>}</form><section className="space-y-3"><h2 className="font-bold">Karya saya</h2>{loading ? <div className="rounded-2xl border border-emerald-950/10 bg-white p-10 text-center text-sm text-muted">Memuat karya...</div> : items.length ? items.map((item) => <article key={item.id} className="rounded-2xl border border-emerald-950/10 bg-white p-5"><p className="font-semibold">{item.title}</p><p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted">{item.type} · {item.category} · {item.status}</p></article>) : <div className="rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-forest/20 bg-white p-10 text-center text-sm text-muted">Belum ada karya yang diunggah.</div>}</section></div>;
}

FILE: src/pages/StudentFines.tsx
================================================
import { ChangeEvent, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useAuth } from "../lib/authContext";
import { supabase } from "../lib/supabase";

type FineRow = { id: string; amount: number; days_late: number; status: "unpaid" | "pending_verification" | "paid"; receipt_url?: string | null; rejection_note?: string | null; loans?: { due_date?: string; books?: { title?: string } } };

export function Fines() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const [fines, setFines] = useState<FineRow[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const load = async () => {
    if (!user) return;
    const { data, error: queryError } = await supabase.from("fines").select("id,amount,days_late,status,receipt_url,rejection_note,loans(due_date,books(title))").eq("user_id", user.id).order("created_at", { ascending: false });
    setError(queryError?.message ?? null);
    setFines((data ?? []) as FineRow[]);
    setLoading(false);
  };
  useEffect(() => { void load(); }, [user]);

  const uploadReceipt = async (fine: FineRow, event: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = event.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file || !user) return;
    setUploading(fine.id);
    const path = `${user.id}/${fine.id}-${Date.now()}-${file.name}`;
    const uploadResult = await supabase.storage.from("qris-receipts").upload(path, file, { upsert: false });
    if (uploadResult.error) setError(uploadResult.error.message);
    else {
      const { error: updateError } = await supabase.from("fines").update({ receipt_url: path, status: "pending_verification" }).eq("id", fine.id).eq("user_id", user.id).eq("status", "unpaid");
      if (updateError) setError(updateError.message);
      else await load();
    }
    setUploading(null);
  };

  if (loading) return <div className="rounded-2xl border border-emerald-950/10 bg-white p-10 text-center text-sm text-muted">Memuat denda...</div>;
  if (error) return <div role="alert" className="rounded-2xl border border-danger/20 bg-danger-soft p-6 text-sm text-danger">{error}</div>;
  const outstanding = fines.filter((fine) => fine.status !== "paid").reduce((sum, fine) => sum + Number(fine.amount), 0);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <header><p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-forest">Keuangan</p><h1 className="mt-1 text-2xl font-black">Denda & pembayaran</h1></header>
      <div className="rounded-2xl border border-emerald-950/10 bg-white p-5"><p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-muted">Total belum lunas</p><p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-bold text-danger">Rp {outstanding.toLocaleString("id-ID")}</p></div>
      {fines.length ? fines.map((fine) => <article key={fine.id} className="rounded-2xl border border-emerald-950/10 bg-white p-5"><div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-4"><div><h2 className="font-bold">{fine.loans?.books?.title ?? "Denda perpustakaan"}</h2><p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted">{fine.days_late} hari terlambat · Rp {Number(fine.amount).toLocaleString("id-ID")}</p></div><span className={`rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold ${fine.status === "paid" ? "bg-success-soft text-success" : fine.status === "pending_verification" ? "bg-accent-soft text-forest" : "bg-danger-soft text-danger"}`}>{fine.status === "paid" ? "Lunas" : fine.status === "pending_verification" ? "Menunggu verifikasi" : "Belum dibayar"}</span></div>{fine.rejection_note && <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-danger">{fine.rejection_note}</p>}{fine.status === "unpaid" && <label className="mt-4 inline-flex cursor-pointer rounded-lg bg-forest px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white">{uploading === fine.id ? "Mengunggah..." : "Upload bukti QRIS"}<input type="file" accept="image/*,application/pdf" className="hidden" onChange={(event) => void uploadReceipt(fine, event)} disabled={uploading !== null} /></label>}</article>) : <div className="rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-forest/20 bg-white p-12 text-center text-sm text-muted">Tidak ada denda.</div>}
    </div>
  );
}

import { QrisMock } from "./QrisMock";

};
export function Icon({ d, className = "size-5" }: { d: keyof typeof P; className?: string }) {
  return (
    <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={1.8} strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" className={className} aria-hidden>
      <path d={P[d]} />
    </svg>
  );
}

export function Badge({ tone, children }: { tone: "green" | "amber" | "red" | "slate" | "lime"; children: ReactNode }) {
  const m = {
    green: "bg-success-soft text-success",
    amber: "bg-amber-100 text-amber-800",
    red: "bg-danger-soft text-danger",
    slate: "bg-slate-100 text-muted",
    lime: "bg-accent-soft text-forest",
  }[tone];
  return <span className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold ${m}`}>{children}</span>;
}
export function Tabs<T extends string>({ value, onChange, items }: { value: T; onChange: (v: T) => void; items: [T, string][] }) {
  return (
    <div className="inline-flex flex-wrap gap-1 rounded-xl border border-emerald-950/10 bg-white p-1">
      {items.map(([k, l]) => (
        <button key={k} onClick={() => onChange(k)} className={`rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold transition ${value === k ? "bg-forest text-white" : "text-muted hover:bg-canvas hover:text-ink"}`}>
          {l}
        </button>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

export function Cover({ t, c, small }: { t: string; c: string; small?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <div className="relative aspect-[3/4] overflow-hidden rounded-lg p-3 text-white" style={{ background: c }}>
      <div className="absolute inset-y-0 left-2 w-px bg-white/20" />
      <p className={`font-semibold leading-tight ${small ? "text-[11px]" : "text-sm"}`}>{t}</p>
      <div className="absolute bottom-3 left-3 h-1 w-8 rounded bg-accent" />
    </div>
  );
}

export function Title({ eyebrow, title, sub }: { eyebrow: string; title: string; sub?: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="mb-6">
      <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-forest-light">{eyebrow}</p>
      <h1 className="mt-1 text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-ink md:text-3xl">{title}</h1>
      {sub && <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted">{sub}</p>}
    </div>
  );
}

export function Stat({ icon, label, value, unit, note, tone = "slate" }: { icon: keyof typeof P; label: string; value: string; unit?: string; note: string; tone?: "slate" | "green" | "amber" }) {
  const dot = { slate: "bg-slate-300", green: "bg-emerald-500", amber: "bg-amber-400" }[tone];
  return (
    <div className={`${soft} p-6 transition hover:border-slate-300/70`}>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <p className="text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-slate-400">{label}</p>
        <span className="text-[#0d372b]/50"><Icon d={icon} className="size-4" /></span>
      </div>
      <p className="mt-5 text-3xl font-light tracking-tight text-[#0d372b] tabular-nums">
        {value}{unit && <span className="ml-1 text-base font-normal text-slate-400">{unit}</span>}
      </p>
      <p className="mt-2 flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-slate-500"><span className={`size-1.5 rounded-full ${dot}`} />{note}</p>
    </div>
  );
}

export function useCountdown(hours: number) {
  const [end] = useState(() => Date.now() + hours * 3600_000);
  const [now, setNow] = useState(Date.now());
  useEffect(() => {
    const i = setInterval(() => setNow(Date.now()), 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(i);
  }, []);
  const s = Math.max(0, Math.floor((end - now) / 1000));
  const pad = (n: number) => String(n).padStart(2, "0");
  return `${Math.floor(s / 86400)}h ${pad(Math.floor((s % 86400) / 3600))}:${pad(Math.floor((s % 3600) / 60))}:${pad(s % 60)}`;
}

/* ---------- Views ---------- */
export const soft = "rounded-2xl border border-slate-200/60 bg-white";



export function Modal({ onClose, children }: { onClose: () => void; children: ReactNode }) {
  useEffect(() => {
    const k = (e: KeyboardEvent) => e.key === "Escape" && onClose();
    window.addEventListener("keydown", k);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", k);
  }, [onClose]);
  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 grid place-items-center bg-slate-900/40 p-4 backdrop-blur-[2px]" onClick={onClose}>
      <div role="dialog" aria-modal className="relative w-full max-w-md rounded-2xl border border-slate-200/60 bg-white p-7 shadow-2xl shadow-slate-900/10" onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
        <button onClick={onClose} aria-label="Tutup" className="absolute right-4 top-4 rounded-lg p-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-slate-700"><Icon d="x" className="size-4" /></button>
        {children}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export function UploadModal({ fine, onClose, onSubmit }: { fine: Fine; onClose: () => void; onSubmit: (file: string) => void }) {
  const [file, setFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [drag, setDrag] = useState(false);
  const [err, setErr] = useState("");
  const pick = (f?: File) => {
    if (!f) return;
    if (!/^image\/|application\/pdf/.test(f.type)) return setErr("Format harus JPG, PNG, atau PDF.");
    if (f.size > 5 * 1024 * 1024) return setErr("Ukuran file melebihi 5 MB.");
    setErr(""); setFile(f);
  };
  return (
    <Modal onClose={onClose}>
      <p className="text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-slate-400">{fine.state === "rejected" ? "Unggah Ulang" : "Konfirmasi Pembayaran"}</p>
      <h3 className="mt-1 text-lg font-semibold text-[#0d372b]">Unggah bukti transfer</h3>
      <p className="mt-1 truncate text-sm text-slate-500">{fine.b.t} · {rupiah(fine.late * FINE_RATE)}</p>
      <form onSubmit={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); if (file) onSubmit(file.name); }}>
        <label
          onDragOver={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); setDrag(true); }}
          onDragLeave={() => setDrag(false)}
          onDrop={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); setDrag(false); pick(e.dataTransfer.files[0]); }}
          className={`mt-5 flex cursor-pointer flex-col items-center rounded-xl border border-dashed px-6 py-9 text-center transition ${drag ? "border-[#0d372b]/40 bg-emerald-50/50" : "border-slate-200 hover:border-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-50/60"}`}
        >
          <input type="file" accept="image/*,application/pdf" className="sr-only" onChange={(e) => pick(e.target.files?.[0])} />
          <span className="grid size-10 place-items-center rounded-full border border-slate-200 text-[#0d372b]/70"><Icon d="upload" className="size-4" /></span>
          {file ? (
            <><p className="mt-3 max-w-full truncate text-sm font-medium text-slate-900">{file.name}</p><p className="text-xs text-slate-400">{(file.size / 1024).toFixed(0)} KB · klik untuk ganti</p></>
          ) : (
            <><p className="mt-3 text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">Tarik struk ke sini atau <span className="text-[#0d372b] underline underline-offset-2">pilih file</span></p><p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-400">JPG, PNG, atau PDF · maks. 5 MB</p></>
          )}
        </label>
        {err && <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-red-600">{err}</p>}
        <div className="mt-6 flex justify-end gap-2">
          <button type="button" onClick={onClose} className="rounded-lg border border-slate-200 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-50">Batal</button>
          <button type="submit" disabled={!file} className="rounded-lg bg-[#0d372b] px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-white hover:bg-[#155f46] disabled:bg-slate-100 disabled:text-slate-400">Kirim untuk Verifikasi</button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </Modal>
  );
}

export function QrisModal({ fine, onClose, onUpload }: { fine: Fine; onClose: () => void; onUpload: () => void }) {
  return (
    <Modal onClose={onClose}>
      <p className="text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-slate-400">Pembayaran Denda</p>
      <h3 className="mt-1 text-lg font-semibold text-[#0d372b]">Bayar dengan QRIS</h3>
      <div className="mt-5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200/70">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-100 px-4 py-2.5">
          <span className="text-sm font-black italic tracking-tight text-slate-900">QRIS</span>
          <span className="text-[10px] font-medium uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400">Bank Aceh Syariah</span>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center px-4 py-5">
          <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">UPT Perpustakaan PNL</p>
          <p className="font-mono text-[10px] text-slate-400">NMID: ID1026318842107</p>
          <div className="mt-3"><QrisMock /></div>
          <p className="mt-3 text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-slate-400">Total Bayar</p>
          <p className="text-2xl font-semibold text-[#0d372b] tabular-nums">{rupiah(fine.late * FINE_RATE)}</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <p className="mt-4 text-sm leading-relaxed text-slate-500">Scan menggunakan M-Banking / GoPay / OVO / Dana, lalu simpan bukti struk dan unggah pada tombol <b className="font-medium text-slate-700">Unggah Bukti</b>.</p>
      <p className="mt-2 truncate text-xs text-slate-400">Denda: {fine.b.t} · {fine.late} hari</p>
      <div className="mt-6 flex justify-end gap-2">
        <button onClick={onClose} className="rounded-lg border border-slate-200 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-50">Tutup</button>
        <button onClick={onUpload} className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg bg-[#0d372b] px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-white hover:bg-[#155f46]"><Icon d="upload" className="size-4" />Lanjut Unggah Bukti</button>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
}

FILE: supabase/schema.sql
