-- ==============================================================================
-- SIPERPUS PNL (Politeknik Negeri Lhokseumawe) Database Schema
-- Following Supabase & Postgres Best Practices:
-- 1. Explicit RLS enabled on all tables in public schema
-- 2. TO authenticated / TO anon instead of deprecated auth.role()
-- 3. Row ownership checks with (select auth.uid()) = user_id
-- 4. UPDATE policies require BOTH using (...) AND with check (...)
-- 5. Role & authorization data stored in profiles table / app_metadata
-- ==============================================================================

create extension if not exists pgcrypto;

-- 1. PROFILES (Extends Supabase auth.users)
create table if not exists public.profiles (
  id uuid primary key references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  nim text unique,
  full_name text not null,
  role text not null default 'student' check (role in ('student', 'librarian')),
  prodi text,
  avatar_url text,
  clear_status boolean not null default true, -- Bebas Pustaka
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  updated_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

-- Enable RLS
alter table public.profiles enable row level security;

-- Policies for profiles
drop policy if exists "Public profiles are viewable by authenticated users" on public.profiles;
create policy "Public profiles are viewable by authenticated users"
  on public.profiles
  for select
  to authenticated
  using (true);

drop policy if exists "Users can update their own profile" on public.profiles;
create policy "Users can update their own profile"
  on public.profiles
  for update
  to authenticated
  using ((select auth.uid()) = id)
  with check ((select auth.uid()) = id);

-- 2. BOOKS (Physical catalog)
create table if not exists public.departments (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  code text not null unique,
  name text not null unique,
  sort_order integer not null default 0
);

create table if not exists public.book_topics (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  parent_id uuid references public.book_topics(id) on delete cascade,
  name text not null,
  slug text not null unique,
  sort_order integer not null default 0,
  unique (parent_id, name)
);

alter table public.departments enable row level security;
alter table public.book_topics enable row level security;

drop policy if exists "Catalog departments are public" on public.departments;
create policy "Catalog departments are public"
  on public.departments for select to public using (true);

drop policy if exists "Catalog topics are public" on public.book_topics;
create policy "Catalog topics are public"
  on public.book_topics for select to public using (true);

create table if not exists public.books (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  code text unique not null,
  title text not null,
  author text not null,
  category text not null,
  department_id uuid references public.departments(id),
  topic_id uuid references public.book_topics(id),
  year int not null,
  stock int not null default 1 check (stock >= 0),
  shelf text not null,
  cover_url text,
  available boolean generated always as (stock > 0) stored,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

alter table public.books enable row level security;

drop policy if exists "Books are viewable by everyone" on public.books;
create policy "Books are viewable by everyone"
  on public.books
  for select
  to public
  using (true);

drop policy if exists "Librarians can insert books" on public.books;
create policy "Librarians can insert books"
  on public.books
  for insert
  to authenticated
  with check (
    exists (
      select 1 from public.profiles
      where profiles.id = (select auth.uid()) and profiles.role = 'librarian'
    )
  );

drop policy if exists "Librarians can update books" on public.books;
create policy "Librarians can update books"
  on public.books
  for update
  to authenticated
  using (
    exists (
      select 1 from public.profiles
      where profiles.id = (select auth.uid()) and profiles.role = 'librarian'
    )
  )
  with check (
    exists (
      select 1 from public.profiles
      where profiles.id = (select auth.uid()) and profiles.role = 'librarian'
    )
  );

drop policy if exists "Librarians can delete books" on public.books;
create policy "Librarians can delete books"
  on public.books
  for delete
  to authenticated
  using (
    exists (
      select 1 from public.profiles
      where profiles.id = (select auth.uid()) and profiles.role = 'librarian'
    )
  );

-- 3. HOLDS (Reservations)
create table if not exists public.holds (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id uuid not null references public.profiles(id) on delete cascade,
  book_id uuid not null references public.books(id) on delete restrict,
  pin text not null,
  status text not null default 'active' check (status in ('active', 'collected', 'expired', 'cancelled')),
  expires_at timestamptz not null default (now() + interval '3 days'),
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

alter table public.holds enable row level security;

drop policy if exists "Users can view their own holds" on public.holds;
create policy "Users can view their own holds"
  on public.holds
  for select
  to authenticated
  using (
    (select auth.uid()) = user_id or
    exists (
      select 1 from public.profiles
      where profiles.id = (select auth.uid()) and profiles.role = 'librarian'
    )
  );

drop policy if exists "Students can reserve books" on public.holds;
create policy "Students can reserve books"
  on public.holds
  for insert
  to authenticated
  with check ((select auth.uid()) = user_id);

drop policy if exists "Hold update allowed by student owner or librarian" on public.holds;
create policy "Hold update allowed by student owner or librarian"
  on public.holds
  for update
  to authenticated
  using (
    (select auth.uid()) = user_id or
    exists (
      select 1 from public.profiles
      where profiles.id = (select auth.uid()) and profiles.role = 'librarian'
    )
  )
  with check (
    (select auth.uid()) = user_id or
    exists (
      select 1 from public.profiles
      where profiles.id = (select auth.uid()) and profiles.role = 'librarian'
    )
  );

-- 4. LOANS (Active borrowings & history)
create table if not exists public.loans (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id uuid not null references public.profiles(id) on delete cascade,
  book_id uuid not null references public.books(id) on delete restrict,
  borrowed_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  due_date timestamptz not null default (now() + interval '14 days'),
  returned_at timestamptz,
  status text not null default 'borrowed' check (status in ('borrowed', 'returned', 'overdue')),
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

alter table public.loans enable row level security;

drop policy if exists "Users can view their own loans or librarians can view all" on public.loans;
create policy "Users can view their own loans or librarians can view all"
  on public.loans
  for select
  to authenticated
  using (
    (select auth.uid()) = user_id or
    exists (
      select 1 from public.profiles
      where profiles.id = (select auth.uid()) and profiles.role = 'librarian'
    )
  );

drop policy if exists "Librarians can insert and update loans" on public.loans;
create policy "Librarians can insert and update loans"
  on public.loans
  for all
  to authenticated
  using (
    exists (
      select 1 from public.profiles
      where profiles.id = (select auth.uid()) and profiles.role = 'librarian'
    )
  )
  with check (
    exists (
      select 1 from public.profiles
      where profiles.id = (select auth.uid()) and profiles.role = 'librarian'
    )
  );

-- 5. FINES (Overdue fines and QRIS payment verification)
create table if not exists public.fines (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id uuid not null references public.profiles(id) on delete cascade,
  loan_id uuid references public.loans(id) on delete set null,
  amount bigint not null check (amount >= 0),
  days_late int not null default 0,
  status text not null default 'unpaid' check (status in ('unpaid', 'pending_verification', 'paid')),
  receipt_url text, -- proof of QRIS payment uploaded to storage
  verified_at timestamptz,
  verified_by uuid references public.profiles(id),
  rejection_note text,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

alter table public.fines enable row level security;

drop policy if exists "Users can view their own fines or librarians view all" on public.fines;
create policy "Users can view their own fines or librarians view all"
  on public.fines
  for select
  to authenticated
  using (
    (select auth.uid()) = user_id or
    exists (
      select 1 from public.profiles
      where profiles.id = (select auth.uid()) and profiles.role = 'librarian'
    )
  );

drop policy if exists "Users can update fine receipt for verification" on public.fines;
create policy "Users can update fine receipt for verification"
  on public.fines
  for update
  to authenticated
  using ((select auth.uid()) = user_id)
  with check ((select auth.uid()) = user_id);

drop policy if exists "Librarians can manage all fines" on public.fines;
create policy "Librarians can manage all fines"
  on public.fines
  for update
  to authenticated
  using (
    exists (
      select 1 from public.profiles
      where profiles.id = (select auth.uid()) and profiles.role = 'librarian'
    )
  )
  with check (
    exists (
      select 1 from public.profiles
      where profiles.id = (select auth.uid()) and profiles.role = 'librarian'
    )
  );

-- 6. REPOSITORY_ITEMS (Theses, capstone, journals)
create table if not exists public.repository_items (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  author_id uuid not null references public.profiles(id) on delete cascade,
  title text not null,
  prodi text not null,
  year int not null,
  abstract text not null,
  pdf_url text,
  file_name text,
  file_size text,
  status text not null default 'pending_curation' check (status in ('pending_curation', 'published', 'revision_needed')),
  reviewer_notes text,
  downloads_count int not null default 0,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

alter table public.repository_items enable row level security;

drop policy if exists "Published repo items viewable by everyone" on public.repository_items;
create policy "Published repo items viewable by everyone"
  on public.repository_items
  for select
  to public
  using (
    status = 'published' or
    (select auth.uid()) = author_id or
    exists (
      select 1 from public.profiles
      where profiles.id = (select auth.uid()) and profiles.role = 'librarian'
    )
  );

drop policy if exists "Students can submit repository items" on public.repository_items;
create policy "Students can submit repository items"
  on public.repository_items
  for insert
  to authenticated
  with check ((select auth.uid()) = author_id);

drop policy if exists "Authors can update pending items" on public.repository_items;
create policy "Authors can update pending items"
  on public.repository_items
  for update
  to authenticated
  using ((select auth.uid()) = author_id)
  with check ((select auth.uid()) = author_id);

drop policy if exists "Librarians can curate repository items" on public.repository_items;
create policy "Librarians can curate repository items"
  on public.repository_items
  for update
  to authenticated
  using (
    exists (
      select 1 from public.profiles
      where profiles.id = (select auth.uid()) and profiles.role = 'librarian'
    )
  )
  with check (
    exists (
      select 1 from public.profiles
      where profiles.id = (select auth.uid()) and profiles.role = 'librarian'
    )
  );

-- 7. AUTOMATIC USER PROFILE TRIGGER
create or replace function public.handle_new_user()
returns trigger
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = ''
as $$
begin
  insert into public.profiles (id, nim, full_name, role, prodi)
  values (
    new.id,
    coalesce(new.raw_user_meta_data->>'nim', null),
    coalesce(new.raw_user_meta_data->>'full_name', 'Mahasiswa PNL'),
    coalesce(new.raw_app_meta_data->>'role', 'student'),
    coalesce(new.raw_user_meta_data->>'prodi', 'Teknologi Informasi & Komputer')
  );
  return new;
end;
$$;

drop trigger if exists on_auth_user_created on auth.users;
create trigger on_auth_user_created
  after insert on auth.users
  for each row execute procedure public.handle_new_user();

-- Initial Seed Data for Siperpus
insert into public.departments (code, name, sort_order)
values
  ('TIK', 'Teknologi Informasi dan Komputer', 1),

  ('TM', 'Teknik Mesin', 3),
  ('TK', 'Teknik Kimia', 4),
  ('TS', 'Teknik Sipil', 5),
  ('BIS', 'Bisnis', 6)
on conflict (code) do update set name = excluded.name, sort_order = excluded.sort_order;

insert into public.book_topics (name, slug, sort_order)
values
  ('Filsafat', 'filsafat', 1),

select parent.id, child.name, child.slug, child.sort_order
from (values

update public.books b
set topic_id = t.id
from public.book_topics t
where t.slug = case
  when b.code like 'INF-%' then 'rekayasa-perangkat-lunak'
  when b.code in ('ELK-019', 'ELK-037') then 'sistem-kendali-otomasi'
  when b.code = 'ELK-022' then 'sistem-tenaga-listrik'
  when b.code in ('MES-005', 'MES-029') then 'termodinamika'
  when b.code = 'MES-018' then 'teknologi-manufaktur'
  when b.code in ('KIM-031', 'KIM-044') then 'proses-industri-kimia'
  when b.code = 'KIM-052' then 'pengolahan-limbah-industri'
  when b.code in ('SPL-014', 'SPL-033') then 'teknologi-beton'
  when b.code in ('AKT-012', 'TNI-027') then 'akuntansi'
  when b.code = 'TNI-041' then 'kewirausahaan'
  else 'ilmu-ilmu-terapan'
end;

-- Comprehensive catalog: two distinct, topic-specific synthetic records per
-- leaf topic. Codes make this block idempotent and safe to rerun.
do $$
declare
  topic_row record;
  department_uuid uuid;
  department_name text;
  code_one text;
  code_two text;
begin
  for topic_row in
    select child.id, child.slug, child.name, parent.slug as parent_slug
    from public.book_topics child
    join public.book_topics parent on parent.id = child.parent_id
    order by parent.sort_order, child.sort_order
  loop
    department_name := case topic_row.parent_slug
      when 'tik' then 'Teknologi Informasi dan Komputer'
      when 'teknik-elektro' then 'Teknik Elektro'
      when 'teknik-mesin' then 'Teknik Mesin'
      when 'teknik-kimia' then 'Teknik Kimia'
      when 'teknik-sipil' then 'Teknik Sipil'
      when 'ilmu-ilmu-terapan' then case topic_row.slug
        when 'tik' then 'Teknologi Informasi dan Komputer'
        when 'teknik-elektro' then 'Teknik Elektro'
        when 'teknik-mesin' then 'Teknik Mesin'
        when 'teknik-kimia' then 'Teknik Kimia'
        when 'teknik-sipil' then 'Teknik Sipil'
        else null
      end
      else null
    end;

    select id into department_uuid
    from public.departments
    where name = department_name;

    code_one := 'CAT-' || topic_row.slug || '-01';
    code_two := 'CAT-' || topic_row.slug || '-02';

    insert into public.books (
      code, title, author, category, department_id, topic_id, year,
      stock, shelf, cover_url
    )
    values (
      code_one,
      'Dasar ' || topic_row.name,
      case topic_row.slug
        when 'pemrograman' then 'Budi Raharjo'
        when 'keamanan-siber' then 'Stallings dan Brown'
        when 'sistem-kendali-otomasi' then 'Katsuhiko Ogata'
        when 'struktur-bangunan' then 'Wang dan Salmon'
        when 'manajemen-pemasaran' then 'Philip Kotler'
        when 'filsafat-ilmu' then 'Jujun S. Suriasumantri'
        when 'sejarah-indonesia' then 'M.C. Ricklefs'
        else 'Tim Koleksi Akademik PNL'
      end,
      coalesce(department_name, 'Koleksi Umum'),
      department_uuid,
      topic_row.id,
      2019 + (abs(hashtext(topic_row.slug)) % 7),
      2 + (abs(hashtext(topic_row.slug)) % 5),
      case
        when department_name = 'Teknologi Informasi dan Komputer' then 'Rak TIK-' || lpad((abs(hashtext(topic_row.slug)) % 20 + 1)::text, 2, '0')
        when department_name = 'Teknik Elektro' then 'Rak TE-' || lpad((abs(hashtext(topic_row.slug)) % 20 + 1)::text, 2, '0')
        when department_name = 'Teknik Mesin' then 'Rak TM-' || lpad((abs(hashtext(topic_row.slug)) % 20 + 1)::text, 2, '0')
        when department_name = 'Teknik Kimia' then 'Rak TK-' || lpad((abs(hashtext(topic_row.slug)) % 20 + 1)::text, 2, '0')
        when department_name = 'Teknik Sipil' then 'Rak TS-' || lpad((abs(hashtext(topic_row.slug)) % 20 + 1)::text, 2, '0')
        else 'Rak UM-' || lpad((abs(hashtext(topic_row.slug)) % 20 + 1)::text, 2, '0')
      end,
      'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1544947950-fa07a98d237f?auto=format&fit=crop&w=400&q=80'
    )
    on conflict (code) do nothing;

    insert into public.books (
      code, title, author, category, department_id, topic_id, year,
      stock, shelf, cover_url
    )
    values (
      code_two,
      'Praktik dan Studi Kasus ' || topic_row.name,
      case topic_row.slug
        when 'basis-data' then 'Abraham Silberschatz'
        when 'jaringan-komputer' then 'Andrew S. Tanenbaum'
        when 'robotika' then 'Bruno Siciliano'
        when 'termodinamika' then 'Yunus A. Cengel'
        when 'teknologi-beton' then 'Nawy dan Winter'
        when 'akuntansi' then 'Mulyadi'
        when 'bisnis-digital' then 'Dave Chaffey'
        else 'Tim Koleksi Akademik PNL'
      end,
      coalesce(department_name, 'Koleksi Umum'),
      department_uuid,
      topic_row.id,
      2020 + (abs(hashtext(topic_row.slug || '-02')) % 6),
      1 + (abs(hashtext(topic_row.slug || '-02')) % 6),
      case
        when department_name = 'Teknologi Informasi dan Komputer' then 'Rak TIK-' || lpad((abs(hashtext(topic_row.slug || '-02')) % 20 + 1)::text, 2, '0')
        when department_name = 'Teknik Elektro' then 'Rak TE-' || lpad((abs(hashtext(topic_row.slug || '-02')) % 20 + 1)::text, 2, '0')
        when department_name = 'Teknik Mesin' then 'Rak TM-' || lpad((abs(hashtext(topic_row.slug || '-02')) % 20 + 1)::text, 2, '0')
        when department_name = 'Teknik Kimia' then 'Rak TK-' || lpad((abs(hashtext(topic_row.slug || '-02')) % 20 + 1)::text, 2, '0')
        when department_name = 'Teknik Sipil' then 'Rak TS-' || lpad((abs(hashtext(topic_row.slug || '-02')) % 20 + 1)::text, 2, '0')
        else 'Rak UM-' || lpad((abs(hashtext(topic_row.slug || '-02')) % 20 + 1)::text, 2, '0')
      end,
      'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1495446815901-a7297e633e8d?auto=format&fit=crop&w=400&q=80'
    )
    on conflict (code) do nothing;
  end loop;
end $$;

-- Repair/reapply the foreign-key relation for generated records. The slug in
-- each deterministic code is the source of truth, so rerunning the seed also
-- corrects records created by an earlier seed version.
update public.books b
set topic_id = t.id
from public.book_topics t
where b.code like 'CAT-%'
  and t.slug = regexp_replace(b.code, '^CAT-(.*)-(01|02)$', '\1');

update public.books b
set department_id = d.id
from public.departments d
cross join public.book_topics t
join public.book_topics parent on parent.id = t.parent_id
where b.code like 'CAT-%'
  and t.id = b.topic_id
  and d.name = case
    when t.slug = 'tik' or parent.slug = 'tik' then 'Teknologi Informasi dan Komputer'
    when t.slug = 'teknik-elektro' or parent.slug = 'teknik-elektro' then 'Teknik Elektro'
    when t.slug = 'teknik-mesin' or parent.slug = 'teknik-mesin' then 'Teknik Mesin'
    when t.slug = 'teknik-kimia' or parent.slug = 'teknik-kimia' then 'Teknik Kimia'
    when t.slug = 'teknik-sipil' or parent.slug = 'teknik-sipil' then 'Teknik Sipil'
    else null
  end;

insert into public.repository_items (author_id, title, author_name, prodi, year, abstract, category, type, status, pdf_url, file_name, file_size)
select p.id, x.title, x.author_name, x.prodi, x.year, x.abstract, x.category, x.type, 'published', x.file_name, split_part(x.file_name, '/', 2), x.file_size
from (values
  ('Implementasi Sistem Monitoring Jaringan Berbasis IoT', 'Muhammad Fajri', 'Teknologi Informasi dan Komputer', 2025, 'Implementasi monitoring jaringan laboratorium.', 'nonfiction', 'Laporan Magang', 'demo/implementasi-monitoring-jaringan-iot.pdf', '4.2 MB'),
  ('Analisis Kuat Tekan Beton Menggunakan Abu Sekam', 'Nurul Aini', 'Teknik Sipil', 2024, 'Analisis material beton berkelanjutan.', 'nonfiction', 'Makalah', 'demo/analisis-kuat-tekan-beton.pdf', '7.8 MB'),
  ('Senja di Pelabuhan Krueng Geukueh', 'Cut Nabila', 'Bisnis', 2025, 'Cerita pendek berlatar Aceh Utara.', 'fiction', 'Cerpen', 'demo/senja-di-pelabuhan-krueng-geukueh.pdf', '2.4 MB')
) as x(title, author_name, prodi, year, abstract, category, type, file_name, file_size)
cross join lateral (select id from public.profiles order by created_at limit 1) p
where not exists (select 1 from public.repository_items r where r.title = x.title);

update public.repository_items
set pdf_url = case title
  when 'Implementasi Sistem Monitoring Jaringan Berbasis IoT' then 'demo/implementasi-monitoring-jaringan-iot.pdf'
  when 'Analisis Kuat Tekan Beton Menggunakan Abu Sekam' then 'demo/analisis-kuat-tekan-beton.pdf'
  when 'Senja di Pelabuhan Krueng Geukueh' then 'demo/senja-di-pelabuhan-krueng-geukueh.pdf'
end,
file_name = case title
  when 'Implementasi Sistem Monitoring Jaringan Berbasis IoT' then 'implementasi-monitoring-jaringan-iot.pdf'
  when 'Analisis Kuat Tekan Beton Menggunakan Abu Sekam' then 'analisis-kuat-tekan-beton.pdf'
  when 'Senja di Pelabuhan Krueng Geukueh' then 'senja-di-pelabuhan-krueng-geukueh.pdf'
end
where title in (
  'Implementasi Sistem Monitoring Jaringan Berbasis IoT',
  'Analisis Kuat Tekan Beton Menggunakan Abu Sekam',
  'Senja di Pelabuhan Krueng Geukueh'
);

-- Demo data is inserted only when the corresponding dashboard-created users exist.
do $$
declare
  student_id uuid;
  librarian_id uuid;
  demo_book_id uuid;
  demo_loan_id uuid;
begin
  select id into student_id from auth.users where email = '2022573010041@siperpus.pnl.ac.id';
  select id into librarian_id from auth.users where email = '198503152010011001@siperpus.pnl.ac.id';
  select id into demo_book_id from public.books where code = 'INF-042';
  if student_id is not null then
    update public.profiles set nim = '2022573010041', full_name = 'Mahasiswa Demo', role = 'student'
      where id = student_id;
    if not exists (select 1 from public.holds where user_id = student_id and book_id = demo_book_id and status = 'active') then
      insert into public.holds (user_id, book_id, pin, status, expires_at)
      values (student_id, demo_book_id, 'PNL-HOLD-DEMO', 'active', now() + interval '3 days');
    end if;
    if not exists (select 1 from public.loans where user_id = student_id and book_id = demo_book_id and returned_at is null) then
      insert into public.loans (user_id, book_id, borrowed_at, due_date, status)
      values (student_id, demo_book_id, now() - interval '10 days', now() + interval '4 days', 'borrowed')
      returning id into demo_loan_id;
    end if;
    if demo_loan_id is not null and not exists (select 1 from public.fines where loan_id = demo_loan_id) then
      insert into public.fines (user_id, loan_id, amount, days_late, status)
      values (student_id, demo_loan_id, 1000, 1, 'unpaid');
    end if;
  end if;
  if librarian_id is not null then
    update public.profiles set nim = '198503152010011001', full_name = 'Pustakawan Demo', role = 'librarian'
      where id = librarian_id;
  end if;
end $$;

import { Hono } from "npm:hono";
import { cors } from "npm:hono/cors";
import { logger } from "npm:hono/logger";
import * as kv from "./kv_store.tsx";
const app = new Hono();

// Enable logger
app.use('*', logger(console.log));

// Enable CORS for all routes and methods
app.use(
  "/*",
  cors({
    origin: "*",
    allowHeaders: ["Content-Type", "Authorization"],
    allowMethods: ["GET", "POST", "PUT", "DELETE", "OPTIONS"],
    exposeHeaders: ["Content-Length"],
    maxAge: 600,
  }),
);

// Health check endpoint
app.get("/make-server-2e23811d/health", (c) => {
  return c.json({ status: "ok" });
});

Deno.serve(app.fetch);

/* AUTOGENERATED FILE - DO NOT EDIT CONTENTS */

/* Table schema:
CREATE TABLE kv_store_2e23811d (
  key TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,
  value JSONB NOT NULL
);

// View at https://supabase.com/dashboard/project/ffkwdjttcwtgsbfxyaem/database/tables

// This file provides a simple key-value interface for storing Figma Make data. It should be adequate for most small-scale use cases.
import { createClient } from "jsr:@supabase/supabase-js@2.49.8";

const client = () => createClient(
  Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL"),
  Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY"),
);

// Set stores a key-value pair in the database.
export const set = async (key: string, value: any): Promise<void> => {
  const supabase = client()
  const { error } = await supabase.from("kv_store_2e23811d").upsert({
    key,
    value
  });
  if (error) {
    throw new Error(error.message);
  }
};

// Get retrieves a key-value pair from the database.
export const get = async (key: string): Promise<any> => {
  const supabase = client()
  const { data, error } = await supabase.from("kv_store_2e23811d").select("value").eq("key", key).maybeSingle();
  if (error) {
    throw new Error(error.message);
  }
  return data?.value;
};

// Delete deletes a key-value pair from the database.
export const del = async (key: string): Promise<void> => {
  const supabase = client()
  const { error } = await supabase.from("kv_store_2e23811d").delete().eq("key", key);
  if (error) {
    throw new Error(error.message);
  }
};

// Sets multiple key-value pairs in the database.
export const mset = async (keys: string[], values: any[]): Promise<void> => {
  const supabase = client()
  const { error } = await supabase.from("kv_store_2e23811d").upsert(keys.map((k, i) => ({ key: k, value: values[i] })));
  if (error) {
    throw new Error(error.message);
  }
};

// Gets multiple key-value pairs from the database.
export const mget = async (keys: string[]): Promise<any[]> => {
  const supabase = client()
  const { data, error } = await supabase.from("kv_store_2e23811d").select("value").in("key", keys);
  if (error) {
    throw new Error(error.message);
  }
  return data?.map((d) => d.value) ?? [];
};

// Deletes multiple key-value pairs from the database.
export const mdel = async (keys: string[]): Promise<void> => {
  const supabase = client()
  const { error } = await supabase.from("kv_store_2e23811d").delete().in("key", keys);
  if (error) {
    throw new Error(error.message);
  }
};

// Search for key-value pairs by prefix.
export const getByPrefix = async (prefix: string): Promise<any[]> => {
  const supabase = client()
  const { data, error } = await supabase.from("kv_store_2e23811d").select("key, value").like("key", prefix + "%");
  if (error) {
    throw new Error(error.message);
  }
  return data?.map((d) => d.value) ?? [];
};

FILE: supabase/migrations/001_fixes.sql

-- SIPERPUS security, circulation and storage fixes.
-- Run after supabase/schema.sql.

create extension if not exists pgcrypto;

alter table public.repository_items
  add column if not exists category text not null default 'nonfiction'
    check (category in ('nonfiction', 'fiction')),
  add column if not exists type text not null default 'Karya Ilmiah',
  add column if not exists author_name text;

alter table public.holds
  alter column expires_at set default (now() + interval '3 days');

create table if not exists public.downloads (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id uuid not null references public.profiles(id) on delete cascade,
  repository_item_id uuid not null references public.repository_items(id) on delete cascade,
  downloaded_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

create or replace function public.record_download(item_id uuid)
returns void
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = ''
as $$
begin
  if (select auth.uid()) is null then raise exception 'Authentication required'; end if;
  if not exists (select 1 from public.repository_items where id = item_id and status = 'published') then
    raise exception 'Published repository item not found';
  end if;
  insert into public.downloads (user_id, repository_item_id)
  values ((select auth.uid()), item_id);
  update public.repository_items set downloads_count = downloads_count + 1 where id = item_id;
end;
$$;
revoke all on function public.record_download(uuid) from public, anon;
grant execute on function public.record_download(uuid) to authenticated;

alter table public.downloads enable row level security;
drop policy if exists "Users can view their own downloads" on public.downloads;
drop policy if exists "Users can create their own downloads" on public.downloads;
create policy "Users can view their own downloads"
  on public.downloads for select to authenticated
  using ((select auth.uid()) = user_id);
create policy "Users can create their own downloads"
  on public.downloads for insert to authenticated
  with check ((select auth.uid()) = user_id);

create or replace function public.handle_new_user()
returns trigger
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = ''
as $$
begin
  insert into public.profiles (id, nim, full_name, role, prodi)
  values (
    new.id,
    new.raw_user_meta_data->>'nim',
    coalesce(new.raw_user_meta_data->>'full_name', 'Mahasiswa PNL'),
    coalesce(new.raw_app_meta_data->>'role', 'student'),
    coalesce(new.raw_user_meta_data->>'prodi', 'Teknologi Informasi & Komputer')
  )
  on conflict (id) do update set
    nim = excluded.nim,
    full_name = excluded.full_name,
    prodi = excluded.prodi;
  return new;
end;
$$;

create or replace function public.protect_profile_update()
returns trigger
language plpgsql
set search_path = ''
as $$
begin
  if (select auth.uid()) = old.id
     and (old.full_name, old.avatar_url) is distinct from (new.full_name, new.avatar_url)
     and (old.nim, old.role, old.prodi, old.clear_status) is distinct from
         (new.nim, new.role, new.prodi, new.clear_status) then
    raise exception 'Students may only update full_name and avatar_url';
  end if;
  if (select auth.uid()) = old.id
     and (old.nim, old.role, old.prodi, old.clear_status) is distinct from
         (new.nim, new.role, new.prodi, new.clear_status) then
    raise exception 'Students may only update full_name and avatar_url';
  end if;
  return new;
end;
$$;

drop trigger if exists protect_profile_update on public.profiles;
create trigger protect_profile_update
  before update on public.profiles
  for each row execute procedure public.protect_profile_update();

drop policy if exists "Users can update their own profile" on public.profiles;
create policy "Users can update their own profile"
  on public.profiles for update to authenticated
  using ((select auth.uid()) = id)
  with check ((select auth.uid()) = id);

drop policy if exists "Users can update fine receipt for verification" on public.fines;
create policy "Users can update fine receipt for verification"
  on public.fines for update to authenticated
  using ((select auth.uid()) = user_id)
  with check (
    (select auth.uid()) = user_id
    and status = 'pending_verification'
    and receipt_url is not null
    and status is distinct from 'paid'
  );

create or replace function public.is_librarian()
returns boolean
language sql
stable
security definer
set search_path = ''
as $$
  select exists (
    select 1 from public.profiles
    where id = (select auth.uid()) and role = 'librarian'
  );
$$;

create or replace function public.expire_holds()
returns integer
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  expired_count integer := 0;
  expired_hold public.holds;
begin
  for expired_hold in
    select * from public.holds
    where status = 'active' and expires_at <= now()
    for update
  loop
    update public.holds set status = 'expired' where id = expired_hold.id;
    update public.books set stock = stock + 1 where id = expired_hold.book_id;
    expired_count := expired_count + 1;
  end loop;
  return expired_count;
end;
$$;

create or replace function public.reserve_book(book_id uuid)
returns public.holds
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  current_user_id uuid := (select auth.uid());
  selected_book public.books;
  created_hold public.holds;
  generated_pin text;
begin
  if current_user_id is null then raise exception 'Authentication required'; end if;
  perform public.expire_holds();
  select * into selected_book from public.books where id = book_id for update;
  if not found then raise exception 'Book not found'; end if;
  if selected_book.stock <= 0 then raise exception 'Book is unavailable'; end if;
  if exists (
    select 1 from public.holds
    where user_id = current_user_id and public.holds.book_id = reserve_book.book_id and status = 'active'
  ) then raise exception 'You already have an active hold for this book'; end if;

  update public.books set stock = stock - 1 where id = book_id;
  loop
    generated_pin := 'PNL-HOLD-' || upper(substr(
      pg_catalog.md5(
        current_user_id::text
        || pg_catalog.clock_timestamp()::text
        || pg_catalog.random()::text
      ),
      1,
      4
    ));
    exit when not exists (select 1 from public.holds where pin = generated_pin);
  end loop;
  insert into public.holds (user_id, book_id, pin, expires_at)
  values (current_user_id, book_id, generated_pin, now() + interval '3 days')
  returning * into created_hold;
  return created_hold;
end;
$$;

create or replace function public.handover_hold(hold_id uuid)
returns public.loans
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  selected_hold public.holds;
  created_loan public.loans;
begin
  if not public.is_librarian() then raise exception 'Librarian access required'; end if;
  select * into selected_hold from public.holds where id = hold_id for update;
  if not found or selected_hold.status <> 'active' then raise exception 'Active hold not found'; end if;
  update public.holds set status = 'collected' where id = hold_id;
  insert into public.loans (user_id, book_id, borrowed_at, due_date, status)
  values (selected_hold.user_id, selected_hold.book_id, now(), now() + interval '14 days', 'borrowed')
  returning * into created_loan;
  return created_loan;
end;
$$;

create or replace function public.return_loan(loan_id uuid)
returns public.loans
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  selected_loan public.loans;
  late_days integer;
  returned_loan public.loans;
begin
  if not public.is_librarian() then raise exception 'Librarian access required'; end if;
  select * into selected_loan from public.loans where id = loan_id for update;
  if not found or selected_loan.returned_at is not null then raise exception 'Active loan not found'; end if;
  late_days := greatest(0, floor(extract(epoch from (now() - selected_loan.due_date)) / 86400)::integer);
  update public.loans
    set returned_at = now(), status = 'returned'
    where id = loan_id
    returning * into returned_loan;
  update public.books set stock = stock + 1 where id = selected_loan.book_id;
  if late_days > 0 then
    insert into public.fines (user_id, loan_id, amount, days_late)
    values (selected_loan.user_id, selected_loan.id, late_days * 1000, late_days);
    update public.profiles set clear_status = false where id = selected_loan.user_id;
  end if;
  return returned_loan;
end;
$$;

create or replace function public.refresh_clear_status(target_user_id uuid)
returns void
language sql
security definer
set search_path = ''
as $$
  update public.profiles
  set clear_status = not exists (
    select 1 from public.fines
    where user_id = target_user_id and status <> 'paid'
  )
  where id = target_user_id;
$$;

create or replace function public.approve_fine(fine_id uuid)
returns public.fines
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  selected_fine public.fines;
begin
  if not public.is_librarian() then raise exception 'Librarian access required'; end if;
  update public.fines
  set status = 'paid', verified_at = now(), verified_by = (select auth.uid()), rejection_note = null
  where id = fine_id and status = 'pending_verification'
  returning * into selected_fine;
  if not found then raise exception 'Fine is not awaiting verification'; end if;
  perform public.refresh_clear_status(selected_fine.user_id);
  return selected_fine;
end;
$$;

create or replace function public.reject_fine(fine_id uuid, note text)
returns public.fines
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  updated_fine public.fines;
begin
  if not public.is_librarian() then raise exception 'Librarian access required'; end if;
  update public.fines
  set status = 'unpaid', rejection_note = nullif(trim(note), ''), verified_at = null, verified_by = null
  where id = fine_id and status = 'pending_verification'
  returning * into updated_fine;
  if not found then raise exception 'Fine is not awaiting verification'; end if;
  return updated_fine;
end;
$$;

create or replace function public.expire_holds()
returns integer
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  expired_count integer := 0;
  expired_hold public.holds;
begin
  for expired_hold in
    select * from public.holds
    where status = 'active' and expires_at <= now()
    for update
  loop
    update public.holds set status = 'expired' where id = expired_hold.id;
    update public.books set stock = stock + 1 where id = expired_hold.book_id;
    expired_count := expired_count + 1;
  end loop;
  return expired_count;
end;
$$;

create or replace function public.lookup_librarian(identifier text)
returns jsonb
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  target_profile public.profiles;
begin
  if not public.is_librarian() then raise exception 'Librarian access required'; end if;
  perform public.expire_holds();
  select * into target_profile
  from public.profiles
  where upper(nim) = upper(trim(identifier))
  limit 1;
  if not found then
    select p.* into target_profile
    from public.profiles p
    join public.holds h on h.user_id = p.id
    where upper(h.pin) = upper(trim(identifier))
    order by h.created_at desc
    limit 1;
  end if;
  if not found then return null; end if;
  return jsonb_build_object(
    'profile', to_jsonb(target_profile),
    'holds', coalesce((select jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(h) || jsonb_build_object('book', to_jsonb(b)))
      from public.holds h join public.books b on b.id = h.book_id
      where h.user_id = target_profile.id), '[]'::jsonb),
    'loans', coalesce((select jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(l) || jsonb_build_object('book', to_jsonb(b)))
      from public.loans l join public.books b on b.id = l.book_id
      where l.user_id = target_profile.id), '[]'::jsonb),
    'fines', coalesce((select jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(f)) from public.fines f where f.user_id = target_profile.id), '[]'::jsonb)
  );
end;
$$;

revoke all on function public.reserve_book(uuid) from public, anon;
revoke all on function public.handover_hold(uuid) from public, anon;
revoke all on function public.return_loan(uuid) from public, anon;
revoke all on function public.approve_fine(uuid) from public, anon;
revoke all on function public.reject_fine(uuid, text) from public, anon;
revoke all on function public.expire_holds() from public, anon, authenticated;
grant execute on function public.reserve_book(uuid) to authenticated;
grant execute on function public.handover_hold(uuid) to authenticated;
grant execute on function public.return_loan(uuid) to authenticated;
grant execute on function public.approve_fine(uuid) to authenticated;
grant execute on function public.reject_fine(uuid, text) to authenticated;
grant execute on function public.lookup_librarian(text) to authenticated;

insert into storage.buckets (id, name, public)
values ('qris-receipts', 'qris-receipts', false), ('repository-files', 'repository-files', false)
on conflict (id) do update set public = excluded.public;

drop policy if exists "Students upload own QRIS receipts" on storage.objects;
create policy "Students upload own QRIS receipts"
  on storage.objects for insert to authenticated
  with check (bucket_id = 'qris-receipts' and (storage.foldername(name))[1] = (select auth.uid())::text);
drop policy if exists "Students read own QRIS receipts" on storage.objects;
create policy "Students read own QRIS receipts"
  on storage.objects for select to authenticated
  using (
    bucket_id = 'qris-receipts'
    and ((storage.foldername(name))[1] = (select auth.uid())::text or public.is_librarian())
  );
drop policy if exists "Students upload own repository files" on storage.objects;
create policy "Students upload own repository files"
  on storage.objects for insert to authenticated
  with check (bucket_id = 'repository-files' and (storage.foldername(name))[1] = (select auth.uid())::text);
drop policy if exists "Authenticated read repository files" on storage.objects;
create policy "Authenticated read repository files"
  on storage.objects for select to authenticated
  using (
    bucket_id = 'repository-files'
    and (
      public.is_librarian()
      or (storage.foldername(name))[1] = (select auth.uid())::text
      or exists (
        select 1
        from public.repository_items ri
        where ri.status = 'published'
          and ri.pdf_url = name
      )
    )
  );

-- Catalog taxonomy: academic departments and hierarchical book topics.

create table if not exists public.departments (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  code text not null unique,
  name text not null unique,
  sort_order integer not null default 0
);

create table if not exists public.book_topics (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  parent_id uuid references public.book_topics(id) on delete cascade,
  name text not null,
  slug text not null unique,
  sort_order integer not null default 0,
  unique (parent_id, name)
);

alter table public.departments enable row level security;
alter table public.book_topics enable row level security;

create policy "Catalog departments are public"
  on public.departments for select to public using (true);

drop policy if exists "Catalog topics are public" on public.book_topics;
create policy "Catalog topics are public"
  on public.book_topics for select to public using (true);

update public.books
set category = case
  when category in ('Teknik Informatika', 'Teknik Informatika (TIK)', 'TIK', 'Teknologi Informasi dan Komputer')
    then 'Teknologi Informasi dan Komputer'
  when category in ('Tata Niaga', 'Tata Niaga / Bisnis', 'Bisnis')
    then 'Bisnis'
  else category
end;

update public.books b
set department_id = d.id
from public.departments d
where b.department_id is null and d.name = b.category;

update public.books b
set topic_id = t.id
from public.book_topics t
where b.topic_id is null
  and t.slug = case
    when b.code like 'INF-%' then 'rekayasa-perangkat-lunak'
    when b.code in ('ELK-019', 'ELK-037') then 'sistem-kendali-otomasi'
    when b.code = 'ELK-022' then 'sistem-tenaga-listrik'
    when b.code in ('MES-005', 'MES-029') then 'termodinamika'
    when b.code = 'MES-018' then 'teknologi-manufaktur'
    when b.code in ('KIM-031', 'KIM-044') then 'proses-industri-kimia'
    when b.code = 'KIM-052' then 'pengolahan-limbah-industri'
    when b.code in ('SPL-014', 'SPL-033') then 'teknologi-beton'
    when b.code = 'AKT-012' then 'akuntansi'
    when b.code = 'TNI-027' then 'akuntansi'
    when b.code = 'TNI-041' then 'kewirausahaan'
    else 'ilmu-ilmu-terapan'
  end;

create index if not exists books_department_id_idx on public.books (department_id);
create index if not exists books_topic_id_idx on public.books (topic_id);

-- Student circulation and E-Repository fixes.
-- Run after 001_fixes.sql.

create extension if not exists pg_cron;

create or replace function public.reserve_book(book_id uuid)
returns public.holds
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  current_user_id uuid := (select auth.uid());
  selected_book public.books;
  created_hold public.holds;
  generated_pin text;
begin
  if current_user_id is null then
    raise exception 'Authentication required';
  end if;

  perform public.expire_holds();
  select * into selected_book
  from public.books
  where id = book_id
  for update;

  if not found then
    raise exception 'Book not found';
  end if;
  if selected_book.stock <= 0 then
    raise exception 'Book is unavailable';
  end if;
  if exists (
    select 1
    from public.holds
    where user_id = current_user_id
      and public.holds.book_id = reserve_book.book_id
      and status = 'active'
  ) then
    raise exception 'You already have an active hold for this book';
  end if;

  update public.books
  set stock = stock - 1
  where id = book_id;

  loop
    generated_pin := 'PNL-HOLD-' || upper(
      substr(
        pg_catalog.md5(
          current_user_id::text
          || pg_catalog.clock_timestamp()::text
          || pg_catalog.random()::text
        ),
        1,
        4
      )
    );
    exit when not exists (
      select 1 from public.holds where pin = generated_pin
    );
  end loop;

  insert into public.holds (user_id, book_id, pin, expires_at)
  values (current_user_id, book_id, generated_pin, now() + interval '3 days')
  returning * into created_hold;
  return created_hold;
end;
$$;

revoke all on function public.reserve_book(uuid) from public, anon;
grant execute on function public.reserve_book(uuid) to authenticated;

create or replace function public.handover_hold(hold_id uuid)
returns public.loans
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  selected_hold public.holds;
  created_loan public.loans;
begin
  if not public.is_librarian() then
    raise exception 'Librarian access required';
  end if;

  perform public.expire_holds();
  select * into selected_hold
  from public.holds
  where id = hold_id
  for update;
  if not found or selected_hold.status <> 'active' then
    raise exception 'Active hold not found';
  end if;

  update public.holds
  set status = 'collected'
  where id = hold_id;
  insert into public.loans (user_id, book_id, borrowed_at, due_date, status)
  values (selected_hold.user_id, selected_hold.book_id, now(), now() + interval '14 days', 'borrowed')
  returning * into created_loan;
  return created_loan;
end;
$$;

revoke all on function public.handover_hold(uuid) from public, anon;
grant execute on function public.handover_hold(uuid) to authenticated;

alter table public.holds
  alter column expires_at set default (now() + interval '3 days');

insert into storage.buckets (id, name, public, file_size_limit, allowed_mime_types)
values
  ('qris-receipts', 'qris-receipts', false, 10485760, array['image/*', 'application/pdf']),
  ('repository-files', 'repository-files', false, 10485760, array['application/pdf'])
on conflict (id) do update set
  public = excluded.public,
  file_size_limit = excluded.file_size_limit,
  allowed_mime_types = excluded.allowed_mime_types;

do $$
begin
  if not exists (
    select 1 from cron.job where jobname = 'expire-library-holds'
  ) then
    perform cron.schedule(
      'expire-library-holds',
      '*/15 * * * *',
      'select public.expire_holds();'
    );
  end if;
end
$$;
